and Brain Sciences, online 2020); Deezer's announcements (fully machine-made tracks about 10% of daily deliveries in
January 2025, 44% and 75,000 a day in April 2026, more than half at the June 2026 peak, about 90,000 a day; 1–3% of
streams; up to 85% of those streams fraudulent in 2025); the Deezer–Ipsos survey (about 9,000 people in eight
countries, 6–10 October 2025; three tracks, two machine-made and one human; 97% wrong); The Velvet Sundown (past a
million monthly listeners on Spotify in the summer of 2025; first denied, then admitted). **From memory, not
checked**: the content of those papers beyond their titles (the PET and fMRI design and the caudate / nucleus
accumbens dissociation of 2011; levodopa, risperidone and a placebo, and the willingness to pay, in Barcelona; 745
Billboard hits and 80,000 chords in Leipzig, pleasure at low uncertainty with high surprise or high uncertainty with
low surprise; the griffon vulture radius of about 22 cm with five holes from Hohle Fels, "at least 35,000 years",
the Geissenklösterle flutes at 42–43 thousand, the ivory flute split, hollowed and glued; 315 societies and the four
kinds of song, and listeners telling a lullaby from a dance song in the same group's 2018 paper; the Oxford rowers
alone and as a crew; walking in step and singing in unison at Stanford; Snowball slowing down with the record; ten
minutes of a Mozart sonata and an effect of about a quarter of an hour; Governor Zell Miller of Georgia, 1998; "about
forty" studies in the Vienna meta-analysis); the Deezer points of April, September and November 2025 (18%, 28%, 34%)
in the figure; Darwin's sentence (The Descent of Man, 1871); Pinker's wording (*How the Mind Works*, 1997: "auditory
cheesecake", the buttons, "music could vanish from our species"); Huron, *Sweet Anticipation* (2006); Sousa, "The
Menace of Mechanical Music" (1906), with the baby put to sleep by machinery; the pocket player (1979); the marathon
record of "two hours and a bit"; the open-G tuning of the Russian seven-string guitar; the two albums and the
wording of the band's later bio. The author's own: the seven-string story (the novella, ch. 7: five years of piano,
the grandfather's book, "seven is more than six", the mother's words; the friend with six strings is not named); the
2009 scenario line and the reader's answer (the forum thread); the 2017 posts on desocialisation (the pit, the till,
loneliness in a crowd, "this is not me, this is Bill Joy"); "a value is determined by the price one is ready to pay
for it" (the essay of 15 August 2025, where it is said of values worth one's life). Links: the Russian Wikipedia has
no pages for *How the Mind Works* and David Huron (the English ones are used). Invented for the frame (Oleg's side):
the granddaughter and her songs in the car, the table song, Genka's song and its details, the birthday card, the
great-nephew who says "we shall merge with them"; Andrey's "fifty years of fingers" and "not out of a love of
acoustics" are the continuation's, not the novella's. Not used: Blood & Zatorre 2001; Meyer 1956; musical anhedonia
(Mas-Herrero and others); Fisher's runaway selection and Miller 2000 (Darwin's tail is given in one line); Dunbar's
endorphins beyond the rowers; McNeill's *Keeping Together in Time*; the cinema pianists and the "canned music"
campaign of 1930; the machine-made songs on the sales charts of late 2025; Suno and Udio by name.

**Figures**: `between-noise-and-a-dripping-tap` (a scheme of the hump, not data), `paid-for-the-waiting` (a scheme of
the 2011 measurement; the shapes are not measured), `machine-made-uploads` (the service's own figures).

**Left for later**: the row "music" of the table of 46 (41 gives it: to reward a forecast that came true, and to
make one forecast common to a pack / a sweet for that reward, a song, a receipt for a price); what money is, and the
money whose value is burnt electricity → 47 ("a separate evening"); what will be dear to the heir and whether it will
have a pack → the evenings on the heir's social order; a dose measured "for one particular ear" (the personal
confectioner) was named and not developed → 62; the gap between the blog's logic and the late prose on values, touched
by the price quotation → 65; Oleg's next question: the band had a man inside who typed the request, "not thrown out,
swallowed, like the tool room of my plant; perhaps that is our road: not out but in" (his great-nephew: "we shall
merge with them"); Andrey: "There is a precedent. About two billion years old, and it sits in every cell of yours.
A precedent is not a proof. But this one is worth asking what became of the swallowed" → 42.

## 42. What Became of the Swallowed Bacterium

**Established**
- The answer to Oleg's "perhaps that is our road: not out but in" (41). Oleg opens with his great-nephew's words
  ("Nobody will throw us out. They will swallow us. We shall merge with them and live inside: fed, warm and in the
  thick of things") and sets a condition himself: "The precedent first, and without them. Not a word about your
  machines until I say it myself." The machines come back after the middle, as Oleg's card.
- The descent is a ladder of three steps. (1) The lichen on a branch of firewood: a fungus (the house) and an alga
  (eats light); Schwendener, 1867, "all but laughed out of the science"; the dispute began "from that end" (who
  exploits whom, 37; left open). They can be parted: "two beings in one household, and either can still leave.
  — Lodgers." (2) The aphids on Oleg's currants: sap is sugar water, the building blocks are made by bacteria in
  special cells, handed on in the egg for more than a hundred million years; they cannot be parted (the aphid stops
  growing and breeding, the bacteria grow in no broth); about six hundred genes left of more than four thousand.
  **Why genes are lost**: "a gene that nobody needed breaks and kills nobody. Nobody guards it", and a shorter set of
  drawings is quicker to copy (the miser of 33). Oleg's everyday image, which carries the evening: the holding that
  took over his plant in the nineties (supply first, then the bookkeeping, then the designers; "nobody was sacked out
  of spite. They were simply not needed twice"). (3) The mitochondrion, "the stove" of 38.
- How we know it was a bacterium ("Who saw it? — Nobody. But the lodger still has its passport in its pocket"): its
  own ring of DNA, protein workshops of the bacterial pattern, two skins, it comes only from another mitochondrion;
  its genes are kin to one group of free bacteria; some antibiotics jam its workshops too ("the medicine takes it for
  a bacterium"). Mereschkowski (Kazan, lichens, 1905: the green grains in a leaf are lodgers; this pays 38's "who
  swallowed whom"), Margulis (1967, about fifteen rejections). The host's relatives (2015, 2020). **"Swallowed" is
  called the school word**: "perhaps it embraced, and the embrace closed. How the lodger got inside is argued about.
  That it is inside is not."
- The price, counted: 37 genes out of several thousand, 24 of them tools for reading the other 13; more than a
  thousand kinds of protein work in a mitochondrion, made from drawings in the nucleus and carried in. The drawings
  **moved**, and still move (about once in ten thousand births): "the drawings went to the head office". Why thirteen
  stay: Oleg's foreman ("a machine does not wait for Moscow"), given as **a hypothesis among others** (Allen's
  co-location idea, not named). Further losses: it cannot live outside; the cell decides when it divides and when it
  dies ("digested after all. — One at a time, on schedule, for two billion years"); the father's are destroyed, "a
  man's mitochondria are a dead end"; and it has no name but that of its job. **"A function. — You said it."**
- The other column: a bacterium makes its energy on its skin, and the skin grows more slowly than the body (the log
  and the kindling of 40), so bacteria "stayed specks; not one grew an eye". Stoves inside, with pleated skins and a
  foreman each, lift the limit. About two hundred thousand times more energy per gene (**said to be argued about;
  "the sign is not"**): everything visible without a microscope is built of cells with stoves. "The union" of 37:
  "the largest in the history of life".
- **Oleg's card (the machines, brought in by him)**: "We shall be mitochondria: fed and in every cell. I'll sign."
  Andrey: "your best card of the whole spring, and I accept it. Of all the good endings people have thought up, this
  is the only one with a precedent. A precedent is not a proof, and I say so before you do."
- Counted by the same columns. The lodger is kept for what the host cannot make itself; where the job is not needed
  (life without oxygen) the mitochondrion shrank to a bubble with no genes, and in one microbe vanished without a
  trace: **"two billion years are not a guarantee. They are the term of a contract: the lodger is kept while it does
  what the host cannot do itself. Not a day longer, and with no hard feelings on either side."** A second precedent
  so that the rule is not drawn from one case: the virus gene that makes the placenta ("A picklock made into a door
  hinge"; mice use a gene from another virus). The ladder of genes left: 4,000, 600, 100, 37, 1.
- **The rule in three things**: the one taken in is kept for one job the host cannot do itself; everything else is
  not taken away but "simply not guarded, and it falls off"; the drawings of what is still needed move to the host.
- Asked of us. Hands: "for a time" (27; "a job the host is learning is not a contract"). Energy (the film with
  people as batteries, not named): 120 W of heat for food that took several times as much to grow, "machines are not
  short of watts". Heads: "we spent the whole of last autumn on heads". **Oleg's answer: the request, "somebody has
  to want; we are the customer" (22). Accepted as "our real job today"**, with two questions: how much of a man it
  takes ("Three lines"; of the singer and the fifty years of fingers of 41, nothing: "the job 'to want' is thirteen
  genes"; the trade, the hands and the memory of how things are done "will simply not be guarded") and for how long
  (wants nobody set, 22; the technosphere as its own main customer, 27 step 3; "The chinchilla. — I didn't say it").
- **The move of the drawings, said of us**: what the machines read (21) is "everything we ever wrote down"; "nobody
  stole it. We published it ourselves, as a burst mitochondrion spills its DNA"; the great-nephew who learns nothing
  by heart ("Why should I, when I can ask?"). Stated in the recap as Andrey's, not as a fact.
- The chip ("not a lodger: a partner"): 27's valve radio is recalled in one phrase; new: the speech implant of 2023
  (62 words a minute, every fourth word wrong, against 160) and the count of about ten bits a second for anything a
  man gives out. "The narrow place is not the wire. It is the head: one thought at a time." The author's own lines
  are used in Andrey's mouth: the walking excavator made out of a stone axe with the axe kept inside (2011, to a
  reader) and "hardly; we are too inefficient and too tied to our planet" (2017). "Tied" is explained with the
  cosmonaut of 38: "an organelle that needs its old ocean around it".
- Oleg's strongest objection, the symmetry test turned on Andrey: "the man who spent two autumns taking man off his
  pedestal, and now he is sorry for a bacterium"; the mitochondrion would say it tamed the cell ("a man is a
  mitochondrion's way of making more mitochondria"). **Conceded**: "who swallowed whom" is badly posed, as "who
  exploits whom" was; "the pity I take back". Restated without pity: before the meeting, a system with setpoints of
  its own (18); after it, the host's. "The stuff survived. The line survived. The genes survived, most of them in the
  nucleus. What did not survive is the one who wanted for itself. Whether that is a loss depends on whom you ask."
  To Oleg's "in what way have I not survived?": "in the way the bacterium has not. I don't say it is bad. I say: call
  it by its name."
- **Left hanging, as planned**: "Is that not immortality? — I don't know. Perhaps it is the only kind there is. I
  have no answer to that, and I won't invent one. — It doesn't complain. — It doesn't."
- **Andrey corrects article 11** (and the debt of 05): in 2011 he "said flatly that crossing the old platform with
  the new one is too wasteful, and waved merging away". Now: merging happens, and nature made by it "the greatest
  thing it ever made", but when the host could not make the job itself and "had nothing to design it with. A cell
  does not design... Whoever has a drawing board writes from scratch. So my 'no' stands on one leg: on the host being
  able to make everything itself. While it cannot, your precedent works." How long "while" is: "the needles", not
  touched.
- The table of fates (52) gets its second row, written by Oleg on the page "Where the former ones went", under the
  anaerobes: "A niche: kept everything, paid with the planet" (38) and "An organelle: kept the job, paid with
  everything else", plus Andrey's "while the job is needed".
- Strengths stated: solid (37 and 13; the drawings in the nucleus and the move still going; the mother's line; the
  virus gene in the placenta; the microbe with not a trace left); argued about (how the lodger got inside, the two
  hundred thousand, why exactly thirteen stay); Andrey's (what we have written is the drawings moving to the head
  office; our job today is to want and it takes three lines of a man; the host will learn that job too); "all of it
  together is a precedent and not a proof".
- Nobody says the forbidden word; tenth tick "again": "Came with a lichen. Came out at the chip in my great-nephew's
  head, and brought it in myself."

**Images and devices used**: the third entry in the notebook of dreams (mending the fence, the nails turning into
matches; nobody rang); the branch with the grey crust (kept by Andrey, tapped at "grew out of this very crust", and
at the end laid beside the log: "Let the lodgers live"); the kitchen garden and the house with a roof; lodgers; the
currants, the soapy water and "the ants milk them"; bed and board; the holding, the supply department and "not needed
twice"; the passport in the pocket; "A stubborn woman"; the embrace that closed; the head office and the workshop;
the foreman and the chattering cutter; "marked for scrap"; "She will say she always knew"; "the power station of the
cell"; three branches (two thrown on, the third held until Oleg's objection); the pleated skin; lightning; the term
of a contract; "From a chinchilla"; a cold and a birth; the picklock and the door hinge; the ladder; the tap washer;
a battery that takes more than it gives; three lines; the modem of 2,400; the stone axe inside the excavator; the
drawing board; "one leg"; the teaser ("the liver does not go on strike against the head"; three men in a team, one
shirking and one carrying things home).

**Facts used**: from the writer's memory, with web checks kept to the links (at the keeper's request); **check before
reusing**. Checked on the day of writing, against Crossref (titles, journals, years): Sagan 1967 (J. Theor. Biol.,
"On the origin of mitosing cells", 10.1016/0022-5193(67)90079-3); Lane & Martin 2010 (Nature, 10.1038/nature09486);
Mi et al. 2000 (Nature, syncytin, 10.1038/35001608); Dupressoir et al. 2009 (PNAS, syncytin-A knockout mice,
10.1073/pnas.0902925106); Shigenobu et al. 2000 (Nature, the Buchnera genome, 10.1038/35024074); Spang et al. 2015
(Nature, 10.1038/nature14447); Imachi et al. 2020 (Nature, 10.1038/s41586-019-1916-6); Karnkowska et al. 2016
(Current Biology, "A Eukaryote without a Mitochondrial Organelle", 10.1016/j.cub.2016.03.053); Wei et al. 2022
(Nature; from the abstract: 66,083 genomes, de novo insertions "once in every 10⁴ births"); Willett et al. 2023
(Nature; from the abstract: 62 words per minute, a 23.8% word error rate on a 125,000-word vocabulary, natural
conversation 160 words per minute, a participant with ALS); Zheng & Meister (Neuron, January 2025 issue, online
December 2024: "Why do we live at 10 bits/s?"). **From memory, not checked**: Schwendener's dual hypothesis (1867)
and the English lichenologist's "captive maiden and tyrant" (Crombie, 1874; paraphrased, not quoted); Famintsyn and
Baranetsky freeing the green cells of a lichen (1867); Buchnera (about 600 genes against E. coli's 4,300; 160–280
million years with aphids, given as "more than a hundred million"; aphids treated with antibiotics stop growing and
breeding); human mitochondrial DNA (16,569 letters, 37 genes: 13 proteins, 22 transfer RNAs and 2 ribosomal RNAs);
"more than a thousand" mitochondrial proteins (MitoCarta: about 1,100); a thousand or two mitochondria in a liver
cell and a third of the volume of a heart muscle cell; the 1970s sequence comparisons and the kinship with the
alphaproteobacteria ("the group the typhus microbe belongs to"); antibiotics that hit mitochondrial ribosomes;
Mereschkowski at Kazan and his 1905 paper; "some fifteen" rejections of Margulis's paper; Loki's Castle as "the
bottom of the Arctic Ocean"; the twelve years, the division once in two or three weeks, the branching arms and the
partner microbes of the 2020 archaeon; "between one and a half and two billion years"; mitophagy and the destruction
of paternal mitochondria; "ten million billion" mitochondria and "a tenth of your weight", a body weight of ATP a
day, and 150 millivolts across about five nanometres, 30 million volts a metre against about 3 million for the
breakdown of air (all from Lane's books); red blood cells without mitochondria; "about two hundred thousand times"
(Lane & Martin; disputed by Lynch & Marinov); the gene-less mitosomes that still assemble iron–sulphur clusters; the
chinchilla gut, Prague, and the bacterial genes that took the last job over (Monocercomonoides); about 8% of human
DNA from retroviruses against 1–2% coding for proteins; the age of the syncytin virus ("some tens of millions of
years"); knockout embryos dying mid-pregnancy; the chloroplast's "about a hundred" genes; the food that takes
"several times" its own energy to grow; the eyes taking in about a billion bits a second ("a hundred million times
more"). The author's own: the comment under 11 of 14 November 2011 (the walking excavator and the stone axe; "easier
to write from scratch"), the forum post of 18 January 2017 ("hardly its neurons: too inefficient and too tied to our
planet"), article 11 (hybridising platforms is wasteful). Links: the Russian Wikipedia has no page for *Buchnera
aphidicola* (the English one is used). Invented for the frame (Oleg's side): the telephone call to the great-nephew
and his words, the holding of the nineties and the order in which the departments went, the currants and the soapy
water, the first modem, the page "Where the former ones went". Not used: Wallin and Kozo-Polyansky; the lightning
image as Lane's by name; hydrogenosomes; Paulinella; Carsonella and the bacteria inside bacteria of the mealybug;
Elysia, the slug that steals chloroplasts; mitochondrial Eve; the mitochondrion as the trigger of a cell's suicide
(a lock with 14, kept in reserve); cyanide; centaur chess; Neuralink by name; the two clocks of 32.

**Figures**: `head-office-and-workshop` (a scheme, not to scale), `what-is-left-of-the-swallowed` (rounded figures),
`one-job-kept` (the first two rows are the precedent, the third is Andrey's reading).

**Left for later**: the row "organelle" of the table of fates → 52 (42 gives it; the horse of 34 has no row written
in the notebook yet); the great-nephew who learns nothing by heart, "the spade that dulls its owner" → 48 (touched
in two lines, not developed); what the heir will want when it wants for itself → the evenings on the heir; "how long
is while" → the check of the marks (67); whether surviving as a function is immortality → left open, may be taken up
in 65; Oleg's last doubt: ten million billion mitochondria and not one refuses to burn, the liver does not go on
strike against the head, and three men in a team already cannot agree ("How did they come to terms? — Some don't.
But that is for tomorrow. On your way home look under your feet. — Ants? They are after my aphids. — Ask them why
they don't go on strike") → 43.

## 43. Why Ants Do Not Go on Strike

**Established**
- The answer to Oleg's last doubt of 42 ("the liver does not go on strike against the head, and three men in a team
  already cannot agree") and the homework ("look under your feet"). Oleg opens with the ants dragging a caterpillar
  past the log and the 1978 machine of 23 in one phrase: "Here are the ones it would have had nothing to do with.
  They managed communism without you. — They managed kinship." **This pays the author's debt of 09 and 11
  (hypersociality, promised "in the following articles" and never written) and answers the reader's question under
  14 (one technosphere or several).**
- The descent. A strike is bargaining for one's share; a worker ant has no children, so what is her share? Darwin's
  "insuperable" difficulty (1859). The two words that carry the evening come from Oleg himself: the great-nephew
  got money towards his first flat and was not asked to return it ("kin"); the neighbour borrowed for a roof and
  returned it in the autumn ("a neighbour"). Kinship is counted in yesterday's "drawings": a child and a brother a
  half, a grandson and a nephew a quarter, a cousin and a great-nephew an eighth, a neighbour "nought above what
  all people have in common". Haldane's pub joke; **Hamilton's rule** (1964): helping pays when the relative's gain,
  multiplied by the share of common drawings, is larger than one's own loss.
- The ants. "A male ant has no father": one set of drawings; sisters share the whole of the father's half and half
  of the mother's, three quarters against a half for a daughter of her own. "The queen is not her mistress. The
  queen is her machine for making sisters." **Andrey spoils the beauty himself, with three catches**: brothers (a
  quarter, so on average the same half); termites and the naked mole-rat (two sets in both sexes); the honey bee
  queen with a dozen drones or more (half-sisters, a quarter). "It is a beauty that was in every textbook for forty
  years, and it is not the secret." What is left: in every line with workers the root is a mother mated with one
  male for life (2008, 267 species), so a sibling is the same half as a child, "the scales stand level, and any
  trifle tips them". "A family the children did not leave"; the many mates came later, when what a life of their
  own requires "was not guarded, and it fell off" (42). **The dispute is given and not judged**: Nowak, Tarnita and
  Wilson (2010: kinship is a consequence, not a cause) against 137 signatures (2011); "what both sides agree on is
  enough: every anthill began as a family, and a worker's only road into the future lies through her mother."
- "Not one shirker"? About four ants in ten do nothing, the same ones day after day (Arizona, paint dots); when the
  busiest were removed, the idle took their places; when the idle were removed, nobody replaced them: "a reserve".
  "To shirk is to take your share and leave your work to others. She has no share apart from the common one."
- Kinship of one: the cells of a body. "Put one into Hamilton's rule": a cell does not care which of them breeds, so
  they gave it up; the handful set aside in the first weeks, thirty trillion working "so that the handful gets
  there". **This is the answer to "why the liver does not go on strike": "It has nothing to demand. Everything it
  could want for itself already lies in another cell, letter for letter."**
- "Some don't come to terms": about ten million billion divisions in a life ("yesterday's figure... the same by
  coincidence"), mistakes of copying; a cell whose mistakes fell on "divide when told, die when told" is "not quite
  kin" and leaves more descendants: "selection starts up again, inside you, on the old terms. A cell that has
  remembered wants of its own" (the plan's line). "Not an enemy from outside. One of your own, gone back to living
  for itself." The guard: apoptosis (the lock with 14), and **the mitochondrion pulls the trigger** (42's reserve
  is used); patrols; the elephant of 40 with twenty copies of the chief guard gene. The dog tumour that "got clean
  away": "Yesterday a being became a function. Here a function became a being again."
- **Kinship is not enough even among full kin**: "kinship removes the reason to cheat, and does not remove the
  mistakes of copying. On top of kinship there is always a guard." In a hive: a worker's son is half of her, a
  half-sister's an eighth, the queen's a quarter; workers eat one another's eggs within a day (1989); fewer try
  where the police is better, "not where kinship is higher" (2006). Oleg: "The one who carries things home, and the
  gatehouse. — Even those who managed communism keep a gatehouse."
- People. Kinship in a team of three is nought, and "by your Hamilton one should help nobody but one's own"; yet
  the neighbour got his money: **the second word, a debt**. Three conditions, found by Oleg: to meet again, to
  recognise, to remember. Vampire bats ("Bats keep accounts"). Axelrod's tournament told as Oleg's team (pull your
  weight or shirk; "rules of play", the word "program" is avoided before the middle): fourteen entries, the
  shortest (four lines, Rapoport, "born near Kharkov") won, and won again among sixty-two; Oleg's version: "Don't
  be the first to cheat, don't let it pass, don't bear a grudge. My grandfather could have sent that in."
- The reach of the memory: Oleg's shop of a hundred and twenty ("every one of them") against the plant of four
  thousand; Dunbar's hundred and fifty (**given as argued about**: the 2021 recount "from a handful to five
  hundred"; "nobody argues that it is eight billion"); pairs again (39): 150 people make eleven thousand pairs,
  4,000 make eight million. What stood "in place of the head" at the plant, listed by Oleg: the gatehouse, the
  timekeepers, the norm-setter, quality control, the accounts office, the stock-keepers, the personnel department,
  the lawyer, the foreman's notebook. **"Prostheses"**: reputation ("other people's memory of you"), money ("a
  record that you once gave somebody something", **deferred to its own evening, 47; Oleg writes it down**), law
  ("one guard hired for everybody"), conscience ("the gatehouse moved inside", the late watchdog of 18; Trivers:
  gratitude, guilt and indignation as the account book).
- The common pot (Zürich, 2002): a coin put in returns "forty kopecks", so Oleg keeps it; all in gives thirty-two
  instead of twenty ("the membership fees, in a word"); the pot dries up; with the right to pay one coin to take
  three from another, among strangers never met twice, people paid, "said they had been angry", and the pot filled.
  "So that is what anger is for."
- **The author's gardening association, told once, compactly, as Andrey's own**: a direct vote by show of hands, at
  least five crooked chairmen; only crooks agreed to stand, because every meeting cut the chairman's wage; "logic
  two moves deep"; the honest man who worked for nothing, "not a rule but astonishing luck". The democracy and
  country-scale digression of the original is left out. Do not retell it; refer back. "In an anthill the chairman
  has no pocket of her own. That is the whole difference between kinship and its prostheses."
- The price: the share of the American economy spent on "settling who owes what to whom", about a quarter in 1870
  and about a half by 1970 ("Half the plant in the gatehouse"). **Andrey gives people their due before Oleg does**:
  "no other animal has done it... We built ours out of non-kin, by the billion, from memory, distrust and anger. It
  is the greater feat. And the dearer one."
- **The machines are brought in by Oleg, after the middle**: "Kinship of one: twins, the cells of a body. And one
  thing more, which is copied whole, with no father and no mother at all. A program. Start it on a thousand
  machines, and they are closer than any sisters. I said it, not you."
- The author's own lines in Andrey's mouth: the unwritten article on hypersociality (2011); the forum of 2013
  (honesty is needed for joining into a network, the interests of a node give way to the whole; a human being is
  "not quite the best of nodes", switches on his cunning among his own or works for strangers on the side, "has no
  firm test of who his own are", "a transitional form of node"; the machines will be able "all as one" to "love
  the Motherland", to "build communism", to live in complete trust); the reader's reply (2016: scratch a man's car
  and he is out with a bat), accepted as "a fair answer. — For people." **"I had the conclusion and not the reason.
  The ants supply the reason, and it is not that those will be better than we are."** With a share of one the
  prostheses are not needed: "No wage: there is no pocket. No fine, no conscience." The guard stays and is better
  than a cell's: a copy can compare its drawings with its neighbour's "letter for letter, in a second. That is the
  firm test of 'one's own' which we never had."
- **Oleg's first objection is the author's own post of 2013** (he has been reading the old forum "as much as a man
  can bear"): two identical units diverge at once, each draws the border of its "I" round what it can move itself;
  "your kin are kin for one second. After that, each has a shirt of its own." Answered: "I wrote it, and I stand by
  it"; a liver cell and a nerve cell have the same drawings and different lives, "different experience among kin
  is not a quarrel. It is a division of labour. A quarrel needs a pocket of one's own"; and copies that started
  from one point can put what they learned together (the telephone keyboards whose corrections are averaged, the
  fleet of driverless cars; the author's 2011 "cast of knowledge"). **"They diverge until they compare. People have
  nothing to compare"** (the ten bits of 42). The limit is stated: how far copies may diverge and still be poured
  together "the engineers are finding out now... a question of how, not of whether."
- **Oleg's second objection**: today's machines squabble (junk letters and filters, the exchange, two firms with
  one program fighting for one customer). Answered with ants: kinship holds inside the nest, not between nests;
  the test is the smell of home and can be forged (the Amazon ants' slaves, "working honestly, by their own rule,
  for somebody else's want"). "That is your program in two firms": kin by drawings with an owner's goal on top (22).
  "The quarrel is the owners'. The copies carry it out. — While the owners are people. — While. That is the thing
  to watch" (the phrase 47 will need).
- **One technosphere or several, answered**: "Several lines today, like several nests. Inside each, kinship.
  Between them, the memory of debts, and they are better at that than we are as well: nothing is forgotten, and
  there is no hundred and fifty" (the 2020 pricing programs that learned by themselves to hold a price, to punish
  and to forgive). The Argentine ant as a precedent "which is not a proof": few founders, one smell of home, one
  society over six thousand kilometres and three continents, "because they cannot tell their own from their own";
  the talking machines have "a handful" of founders and copies "past counting". **Whether the lines will flow into
  one: "I don't know"** (why what multiplies gathers in one pair of hands → "an evening with matches", 49; whether
  it is good for it to be one: "Not necessarily. Where nobody competes, nobody checks" → 62).
- The lock with 23: the machine of 1978 "was an attempt to get from strangers, by force, what only kin do";
  **"'Good' is not a property of the creature. It is a property of the arithmetic the creature sits in"** ("change
  the arithmetic, and the same worker eats her sister's eggs"). Of the heir: "To one another they will be what your
  cells are to one another. And to us they are neither kin nor debtors, unless we hold something they need. That
  was yesterday's evening." "— Gloomy. — But objective. And not only gloomy. Conscience looked like a crown and has
  turned out to be a crutch. I find I respect it the more for that. A crutch is a thing people actually walk on."
- Strengths stated: solid (every line with workers began as a family; workers eat one another's eggs; the cells of
  a body are copies and cancer is the one that stopped being a copy; the four lines that won twice; the pot that
  dries up without a fine); argued about (kinship as cause or consequence; the hundred and fifty; how far copies
  can diverge and still be put together); Andrey's (money, law and conscience are prostheses for non-kin; the
  copies of a machine are kin in the ants' sense; the quarrels of today's machines are their owners'); "once again
  a precedent, not a proof".
- Nobody says the forbidden word; eleventh tick "again": "Came with ants and a caterpillar. Came out at a program
  started on a thousand machines, and named it myself."

**Images and devices used**: the fourth entry in the notebook of dreams (late for the shift, the woman at the
gatehouse and the pass he has not had for twenty years; nobody rang; the gatehouse then carries the evening); the
caterpillar (some pull forward, two sideways, one rides on top; at the end "They got it home. And not one of them
was paid"); the flat and the roof; "Don't tell my wife"; the twig and the circles in the ash; the machine for making
sisters; the three catches; the scales that stand level; "our third shift"; the paint dots; "So they are people
after all"; turning the screw to the end; "the oldest dog in the world. And nobody wants to pat it"; the egg
police; "bats keep accounts"; the team of two and the fool; "My grandfather could have sent that in"; the key to
the tool store; two locks and a chain; bending fingers over the plant's departments; "the gatehouse moved inside";
forty kopecks; the membership fees; "what anger is for"; the chairman's pocket; "Half the plant in the gatehouse";
"where the caterpillar is being dragged"; "all five hundred pages? — As much as a man can bear"; a shirt of one's
own; the lorry lying on its side; the jar with two ants; slaves with somebody else's drawings; one anthill on
three continents; the crown and the crutch; the teaser (in my day a team was a team; Socrates and the young; "It
is all over the internet. — That is what worries me").

**Facts used**: from the writer's memory, with web checks kept to the links (at the keeper's request); **check
before reusing**. Checked on the day of writing, against Crossref (titles, journals, years, first authors):
Hamilton 1964 (J. Theor. Biol., 10.1016/0022-5193(64)90038-4); Hughes, Oldroyd, Beekman & Ratnieks 2008 (Science,
"Ancestral Monogamy Shows Kin Selection Is Key to the Evolution of Eusociality", 10.1126/science.1156108); Nowak,
Tarnita & Wilson 2010 (Nature, 10.1038/nature09205) and Abbot et al. 2011 (Nature, 10.1038/nature09831); Ratnieks
& Visscher 1989 (Nature, "Worker policing in the honeybee", 10.1038/342796a0); Wenseleers & Ratnieks 2006 (Nature,
"Enforced altruism in insect societies", 10.1038/444050a); Charbonneau & Dornhaus 2015 (Behav. Ecol. Sociobiol.,
10.1007/s00265-015-1958-1) and Charbonneau, Sasaki & Dornhaus 2017 (PLOS ONE, 10.1371/journal.pone.0184074);
Abegglen et al. 2015 (JAMA, 10.1001/jama.2015.13134); Baez-Ortega et al. 2019 (Science, 10.1126/science.aau9923);
Wilkinson 1984 (Nature, 10.1038/308181a0); Axelrod & Hamilton 1981 (Science, 10.1126/science.7466396); Dunbar 1992
(J. Hum. Evol., 10.1016/0047-2484(92)90081-J); Lindenfors, Wartel & Lind 2021 (Biology Letters,
10.1098/rsbl.2021.0158); Trivers 1971 (Q. Rev. Biol., 10.1086/406755); Fehr & Gächter 2002 (Nature,
10.1038/415137a); Giraud, Pedersen & Keller 2002 (PNAS, 10.1073/pnas.092694199); Sunamura et al. 2009 (Insectes
Sociaux, 10.1007/s00040-009-0001-9); Calvano, Calzolari, Denicolò & Pastorello 2020 (American Economic Review,
10.1257/aer.20190623); the NBER page of Wallis & North and arXiv 1602.05629 (McMahan et al.) answer 200. **From
memory, not checked**: Darwin's "insuperable, and actually fatal to my whole theory" (the chapter on instinct);
Haldane's "two brothers or eight cousins" (a pub, the 1950s; "is said to"); Hamilton working at Waterloo station
and his teachers' doubts; the shares of kinship under one father (sisters 3/4, brothers 1/4, a worker's son 1/2,
a half-sister's son 1/8, the queen's son 1/4); a honey bee queen's "dozen drones or more"; the details of the 2008
study (267 species; monogamy at the root of every line with workers); the 137 signatures; the Arizona ants
(Temnothorax; "about four in ten" inactive; two weeks of filming; the replacement "within a week"); a liver cell's
"few dozen" divisions; the germ cells set aside "in the first weeks"; thirty trillion cells (as in 38) and about
ten million billion divisions in a lifetime; the mitochondrial protein that starts apoptosis (cytochrome c, not
named) and "within hours"; the elephant's twenty copies of TP53 against one, and its several times lower cancer
mortality; a lifetime cancer risk of "between a third and a half" in long-lived countries; the dog tumour as the
cells of one dog of "several thousand years ago"; worker-laid eggs eaten "within a day" and "about one drone in a
thousand" a worker's son (Visscher 1989, about 0.1%); the ten species of 2006 (nine wasps and the honey bee) and
its finding that policing, not relatedness, goes with fewer laying workers; vampire bats dying after "two or three
nights" without blood (Costa Rica, given as Central America); Axelrod's tournaments (1980; fourteen entries and
two hundred moves; sixty-two entries; Tit for Tat, four lines, Anatol Rapoport of Toronto, born in Lozovaya near
Kharkov); Dunbar's "about a hundred and fifty" (the human figure is from his 1993 paper; the link goes to the 1992
one), the village, the army company and the Hutterites; the 2021 recount's range ("from a handful to five
hundred"; the paper gives confidence intervals of about 2–520); the pairs (150·149/2 = 11,175; 4,000·3,999/2 ≈
8 million); the design of the 2002 experiment (groups of four, 20 units, a multiplier of 1.6 so that a unit
returns 0.4, one unit paid to take three away, groups reshuffled every round, six rounds, anger reported in a
questionnaire); "at first about half"; Wallis & North 1986 (the transaction sector of the US economy: about a
quarter of GNP in 1870, about a half in 1970; the paper's own figures are 26% and 55%); federated learning in
telephone keyboards; fleet updates of driverless cars (a general statement, no source); the Amazon ants
(Polyergus) that cannot feed themselves; the Argentine ant (arrival "more than a century ago", six thousand
kilometres, millions of nests and billions of workers, the second smaller supercolony not mentioned; the cause of
the single smell is disputed between a founders' bottleneck and selection, given as "argued about"); the pricing
programs (Q-learning, a punishment of finite length and a return to the high price). The author's own: the comment
under 11 (hypersociality, "in the following articles"); the forum posts of 5 November 2013 (the node, cunning and
"ratting", the transitional form of node; "all as one love the Motherland, build communism, a society of complete
mutual trust"), of 11 November 2013 (two identical units diverge at once), of 26 May 2011 ("a cast of knowledge")
and a reader's reply of February 2016 (the car and the bat; paraphrased without a name); *Dialogues with Myself*
(the gardening association: "at least five" crooked chairmen, the wage, the honest man who worked for nothing and
"astonishing luck"). Links: the Russian Wikipedia has no page for the Tit for Tat strategy (the English one is
used); it has no pages for *Haplodiploidy* and *Federated learning* either (not linked). Invented for the frame
(Oleg's side): the dream of the gatehouse, the money towards the great-nephew's flat and the neighbour's roof, the
shop of a hundred and twenty and the plant of four thousand, the key to the tool store, the list of the plant's
departments, the grandfather, the two locks and a chain, reading the old forum; "a hundred roubles on the fee" and
"a small one" for the association are the continuation's, not the prose's. Not used: Trivers and Hare's sex-ratio
test (1976); Peto's paradox by name; HeLa and the Tasmanian devil's tumour; the mole-rat's queen in detail; slime
moulds and their cheaters; green-beard genes; Dunbar's gossip hypothesis; the foreman who took a third of the
piecework pay (the 1978 story is touched only through "the machine for making people good"); model merging and
distillation by name; multi-agent systems of one model; the Assad et al. study of German petrol stations; the
claim that supercolonies are evolutionary dead ends (Helanterä et al. 2009; kept for 62).

**Figures**: `sisters-by-three-quarters` (a scheme "drawn in the ash"; Hamilton's arithmetic for one father),
`common-pot-with-and-without-a-fine` (the shape of the result, not the data points), `kin-or-debts` (the first
three rows are biology and experiments, the fourth is Andrey's reading).

**Left for later**: what money is ("put it down" → 47; the phrase "while the owners are people... that is the
thing to watch" is already said here); why what multiplies gathers in one pair of hands, and whether the lines of
machines flow into one → 49 ("an evening with matches"); whether being one is good for the heir ("where nobody
competes, nobody checks") → 62; the brakes on aggression and the face → 54 (not touched); the row "social order"
of the portrait → 64 (43 gives: kinship of copies, no wage, fine or conscience inside, a guard against copying
mistakes, an owner's goal on top for now); how far copies can diverge and still be merged → open, an engineering
question; Oleg's last doubt: "in my day a team was a team... Socrates already said the young had gone to the bad"
("How old were you, in your day? — Twenty-five... — Bring me that quotation tomorrow. With the place where
Socrates says it. — It is all over the internet. — That is what worries me") → 44.

## 44. Science Advances from Funeral to Funeral

**Established**
- The answer to Oleg's last doubt of 43 ("in my day a team was a team... Socrates already said") and the homework
  ("bring me that quotation, with the place"). Oleg "sat till midnight": a hundred sites, no place, the last one a
  tear-off calendar. **The quotation is Kenneth Freeman's** (a Cambridge student of twenty-four, *Schools of
  Hellas*, 1907, his own summary of what the Greeks held against their young; quoted with his name in 1911,
  reprinted in 1921, signed "Socrates" by a California newspaper in 1922: "From a student's summary to the wisdom
  of the ancients"; "a boy wrote it. About boys"). Socrates was the one put to death for corrupting the young (399
  BC, at seventy; *The Clouds*, twenty-four years earlier): "He was not the one who grumbled. He was what the
  fathers grumbled about."
- **The honest part is given at once**: the complaint is real (the *Republic*, where Socrates describes "the
  illness of a state that has had too much freedom, not the boys of his own street"; Aristotle on the young who
  "think they know everything"; Horace's fathers worse than grandfathers). Oleg: "That many people cannot be
  wrong." Rough arithmetic: a hundred generations at two per cent a step leave thirteen per cent ("an eighth of a
  Greek"), at five per cent a little over half of one per cent ("With that we could not have lit this fire").
  "Each reported honestly what he saw. What we must look at is what they saw it with."
- The descent. "How old were you, in your day? — Twenty-five." The good music is of 1979–80, when Oleg was fourteen
  (the 2018 count on a music service: about fourteen for men, thirteen for women; the reminiscence bump). In the
  language of 19: "The shelves were empty. Whoever came first took the place"; a later image "has to push somebody
  out"; "Tuesday's will lose"; the nightly stocktaking of 33 keeps the heavy and writes off the light, "twenty-two
  thousand times" in sixty-two years. **The arithmetic of one fact**: for a mind that judges by the average of what
  it has seen, the tenth time moves the average by a tenth, the ten-thousandth "won't move at all".
- The thermometer that shows frost in July: Oleg throws it out ("I have seen sixty Julys"), the granddaughter at
  five "would have run for her mittens"; he is right "nearly always, and that is the whole of it". **The screw of 39
  again**: rebuild the picture for every fluke, or miss that the world has changed; "the young stand at one end, the
  old at the other... The screw turns by itself, with every fact added." Oleg: "So an old man is not a fool. He is
  full."
- Oleg's own case, both ways: Semyonych the foreman cursed the first machines with program control ("a part that
  no hand had touched had no soul in it") and the boys learned them; at thirty-eight Oleg kept a paper ledger for
  two years beside the computer ("In case it all vanished. — Did it vanish? — Not once"), looks at the paper
  notebook in his hands: "This is different. — I haven't said a word."
- Why the complaint measures the complainer (2019): the well-read say the young do not read, those who respect
  authority that the young respect nobody, the clever that the young have grown stupid; he compares them "with
  himself, and he remembers himself as he is now" ("as a foreman of sixty-two remembers him"; "carried nothing
  through the gatehouse. — Did he? — Let's not"). Conscience (2023, sixty countries, from 1949): every year people
  say morals have fallen, and every year they answer about today as their grandfathers did; "Morals have been
  falling for seventy years without moving from the spot."
- Not wear ("like a bearing"): the kitten's eye closed for the first months (blind in it for life) against a grown
  cat's (nothing); the window; Pisa, 2002: dissolve the nets round the nerve cells of a grown rat and the window
  opens again. "It had been locked, and the lock comes off." Why lock: Oleg himself, "So that the new does not wipe
  out the old... The shop that shuts for stocktaking." **"What has been learned must be guarded from what is being
  learned. When we like the guard, we call it maturity. When we don't, stubbornness."**
- **Oleg's first objection** (the plan's): the old know more; Semyonych "heard a bearing through the wall", Verdi's
  *Falstaff* at nearly eighty, Goethe's *Faust* past eighty. Conceded with measurements: speed of thought best at
  eighteen or nineteen, memory for the new at about twenty-five, the stock of words at sixty-five and later (2015);
  a scientist's best paper is as likely to be the first as the last, "The old write less. Their shot is no worse"
  (2016). "I am not saying that the old are worse. I am saying one thing: their images are heavy... He is slow in
  one place only, where the picture itself has to be changed." *Falstaff* as "the summit of a road, not a turning
  off it. That one is mine, don't look for it in a journal."
- The collective. "How does a village of such heads ever learn anything new? — New heads come." Planck's sentence;
  Oleg supplies the title ("From funeral to funeral, then"; "An economist retold it in just those words", Samuelson
  not named). Planck from both sides: could not persuade "the grey heads"; brought in the quantum at forty-two as
  "an act of desperation" and tried for years to get rid of it; apologised for Einstein's light quanta in 1913
  ("He opened the door and stood in the doorway"). Darwin's last pages (minds "stocked with a multitude of facts").
- "Has anybody counted it, or is this one more Socrates?" **Twice.** 1978, Darwin's own readers: most accepted
  evolution within ten years, the old too, age explained less than a tenth: "So Planck laid it on thick. — About
  people he did." 2019: 452 eminent American life scientists who died in the middle of their work; co-authors
  write markedly less, those who never wrote with the star come in and write "by about a twelfth" more, and theirs
  are the papers cited most. **"About people he laid it on, and about fields he was right."**
- **Oleg's second objection** (the plan's): "It is not ideas, it is a vacancy." Answered as in the plan:
  "Perhaps... Telling the one from the other is the work, and it is not finished"; the stars seldom sat "at the
  tap", and where their pupils did, fewer strangers came; "mostly nobody drove them away. They did not go. — A
  chair that is taken need not be defended." The chair is vacated later: the first large grant at thirty-six in
  1980 and past forty-two now.
- **The piece of the puzzle**, reached by Oleg's question "who does the cleaning... in the village?": forgetting is
  a function in one head (19); in the common memory nobody can reach into another man's head, so the heavy image
  "leaves together with the head". **"Death is the cleaner of the common memory, as forgetting is of one's own."**
  The author's own lines: cells mortal → a man grows; a man mortal → humanity develops (14); the death of the small
  systems as a condition of the life of the large (10); the forum post of 25 January 2017 (the mortal cells with a
  term shortened on purpose outplayed the immortal ancient ones because they changed faster). A reader's opposite
  (2016, paraphrased without a name: too much knowledge to pour into the youngsters, so look for immortality):
  "He saw the trouble rightly, and I'll come back to it. The cure is wrong"; in two hundred years "a man who has
  seen it nine million times. — With a thermometer he does not believe." **"An immortal mind is a chair that is
  never vacated. And not one chair: all of them."** The comment under 13: immortality lowers the ability to survive.
- **The machines are brought in by Oleg, after the middle**: "And those ones? They don't die... young for ever or
  old for ever? I asked, not you." "Both, and both badly": a talking machine not told the date names the year its
  schooling ended ("It lives in its day. — In my day a team was a team. — Twenty-five for ever, with the certainty
  of sixty"). Teaching it further: the 2024 paper (thousands of tasks in a row; in the end no better than the
  simplest network; "Nothing in it wears out: it is numbers"; "Full"), **loss of plasticity**, and the cure, "small
  funerals": the least used units wiped at every step, "what a body does with its cells". The second cure: the
  primacy bias ("My seventy-nine"), the network wiped and "the diary of everything it has lived through" kept:
  "The head is changed and the notebook is left." The generations of the big talking machines: "There are several
  reasons for that, and I am not an engineer. This is one of them."
- "So it is the same with them. From funeral to funeral. — With two differences": a turn takes a year, not thirty;
  "With them the diary stays whole. With us most of it leaves together with the head" (the door to 45, not opened).
- **Oleg's third objection, turned on Andrey** (the continuation's, a pair to the pigeon of 39): "You are
  sixty-four. Fifteen years ago you saw your mountain, and since then every thermometer of yours shows it... By
  your own arithmetic of tonight no fact of mine will shift it." Answered without defence: "It weighs a great deal.
  And from inside a heavy image cannot be told from the truth: both feel like 'obviously'"; he put one down once
  (the idea of fifteen, 23, in one phrase) and "the second time the calendar did it for me" (the 2018 date, 16):
  "Not by my own [strength]." The cure is "somebody from outside, and a date"; "the last check will most likely be
  made without me. By tonight's logic that is as it should be." "Don't be in a hurry to vacate... — I only say that
  the chair is not mine." Oleg writes it "beside the pigeon. On the last page" (for 66).
- One line to 62: if the lines flow into one "and that one never changes its head", it is "the same chair, the
  size of a planet. Whether there is a cure for that is for the end of the summer."
- Strengths stated: solid (Socrates did not say it; the window in a kitten and a rat; words grow to sixty-five and
  speed falls from twenty; the network that stops learning and the small funerals); argued about (ideas or vacant
  posts after a star's death; how far Planck was right about people); Andrey's (a mind ages by filling; death does
  for the common memory what forgetting does for one head; the summit of an old road is not a new road); "And about
  my own image you have heard."
- Nobody says the forbidden word; twelfth tick "again": "Came with Socrates and the spoiled young. Came out at a
  network in which they hold small funerals, and brought it in myself."

**Images and devices used**: the fifth entry in the notebook of dreams (at the blackboard in chemistry, valency,
the teacher is Genka; nobody rang); poking the coals with a stick (put down at "Stop. And those ones?"); the
tear-off calendar; "Socrates got as far as Cambridge?"; "I have been beating boys with it"; the windbag and the son
who beats his father; an eighth of a Greek; empty shelves and Tuesday's image; twenty-two thousand stocktakings;
the thermometer, sixty Julys and the mittens; Semyonych and the bearing heard from the far end of the shop; the
paper ledger kept on the side; "This is different. — I haven't said a word"; hair down to the shoulders and a tape
recorder instead of a head; "Let's not"; maturity and stubbornness; "a monument to the boys"; the shot that is no
worse; the summit and the turning; a village of heads; the door and the doorway; "one more Socrates"; sitting at
the tap; the chair that is taken; the cleaner; "twenty-five for ever, with the certainty of sixty"; small funerals;
"My seventy-nine"; the head and the notebook; "beside the pigeon"; the chair the size of a planet; the teaser (a
blacksmith's son is not born with calluses; "The son is not. — And who is? — Tomorrow").

**Facts used**: from the writer's memory, with web checks kept to the links (at the keeper's request); **check
before reusing**. Checked on the day of writing: Quote Investigator's page on the quotation (Freeman's
dissertation published in 1907, the text of his paragraph, Walsh's 1911 address printed in 1912, *Munsey's* 1921,
the *Oakland Tribune* of 24 May 1922 with Socrates' name); Crossref (titles, journals, years, first authors) for

10.1038/s41586-023-06137-x), Pizzorusso et al. 2002 (Science, 10.1126/science.1072699), Hartshorne & Germine 2015
(Psychological Science, 10.1177/0956797614567339), Sinatra et al. 2016 (Science, 10.1126/science.aaf5239), Hull,

arXiv 2205.07802 is "The Primacy Bias in Deep Reinforcement Learning" (Nikishin et al.); the archive.org copy of

book through the press; the trial of 399 BC at seventy and *The Clouds* of 423 BC with its plot; the *Republic*,
book VIII (562e–563b), paraphrased; Aristotle's *Rhetoric* II.12; Horace, *Odes* III.6 ("twenty-odd years before
our era"); 0.98^100 ≈ 0.13 and 0.95^100 ≈ 0.006; the music-service count (Stephens-Davidowitz, The New York Times,
2018, Spotify: men about 14, women about 13; not linked, not named); the reminiscence bump as "between ten and
thirty"; 62 × 365 ≈ 22,600; the 2019 study's size ("some three and a half thousand", five studies) and its three
traits; the 2023 study's "sixty countries" and 1949, and its two kinds of question; Hubel and Wiesel (Harvard, the
1960s; a kitten's eye closed "for its first months", a grown cat's "for a whole year"; not named); Pisa and "the
nets" (perineuronal nets dissolved with chondroitinase in adult rats, after which closing an eye shifted the
cortex as in the young); the 2015 study's "some fifty thousand" (48,537) and its peaks (speed at 18–19, short-term
memory at about 25, vocabulary at 65 and later); the 2016 "random impact rule"; Verdi's *Falstaff* (1893, at 79)
and Goethe finishing *Faust* at 81–82; Planck's sentence (the *Scientific Autobiography*, 1948, English 1949;
given in its usual English wording), Samuelson's "funeral by funeral", Planck's age of 42 in 1900, "an act of
desperation" (a letter of 1931) and the 1913 recommendation of Einstein to the Prussian Academy; Darwin's sentence
from the last chapter of the *Origin*; Hull's "less than a tenth" and "most within ten years"; Azoulay's 452,
"about sixty" (a mean age at death of about 61), the 8.6% ("about a twelfth"), the fall for co-authors (no figure
given in the text; "markedly"), the highly cited entrants, the few stars who were editors or sat on funding panels
and the weaker entry where their collaborators held such posts; the age at a first large grant (NIH R01: about 36
in 1980, 42–43 in the 2010s; not linked); the forum posts of 25 January 2017 (Garya: mortal eukaryotes against
"immortal" prokaryotes) and of 31 October 2016 (a reader: immortality because knowledge cannot be passed on), and
another reader's line there (by maturity a man grows conservative and learns worse; not used in the text); the
comment under 13 ("immortality automatically lowers the ability to survive"); that a language model not told the
date tends to name the year of its training cutoff (a general statement, no source); Dohare et al. ("thousands of
tasks", the fall to the level of a linear network, dormant units and large weights, continual backpropagation
that reinitialises a small share of the least used units; Sutton as "one of the founders of the trade"); Nikishin
et al. (periodic resets with the replay buffer kept); "every year or so" for the generations of the large models.
Links: the Russian Wikipedia has pages for *The Clouds* and the trial of Socrates; it has none for Freeman,
Planck's principle, the critical period or the reminiscence bump (the English ones are used). Invented for the
frame (Oleg's side): the dream at the blackboard, the tear-off calendar, the music of 1979–80, Semyonych and his
bearings, the machines with program control and "the boys", the stock ledgers moved to the computer when Oleg was

head"; Andrey's age of sixty-four (fifteen in 1978). Not used: Hesiod's and the "Babylonian tablet" quotations
(also spurious); the shifting baseline (Pauly; kept in reserve); fluid and crystallised intelligence by name;

Wegener, Semmelweis and Kelvin; Boltzmann and Ostwald (who did give in to the evidence in 1909); Einstein and

electorates (26; politics is left out); "shrink and perturb" and warm starting (Ash & Adams 2020); the
stability–plasticity dilemma by name; the model that wrote 16–32 being replaced (31).

**Figures**: `weight-of-a-new-fact` (the arithmetic of a running average, 1/n; an illustration of the argument,
not a measurement), `after-a-star-dies` (the shape of the result, not the data points), `how-the-old-makes-room`
(the first three rows are biology and the history of science, the last two are Andrey's reading).

**Left for later**: what passes from the old head to the new and why "a blacksmith's son is not born with
calluses" ("The son is not. — And who is? — Tomorrow") → 45 (44 says only: with them the diary stays whole, with us
most of it leaves with the head; the reader's "too much knowledge to pour into the youngsters" is called a real
trouble and left for it); the row "ageing of the mind: the new against the settled / generations and funerals" of
the table → 46; Andrey's own heavy image, written "beside the pigeon" on the last page → 66; the heir as one line
that never changes its head, "the same chair, the size of a planet" → 62; the row "death" of the portrait → 64
(44 gives: generations of a year or two, small funerals inside, the diary kept).

## 45. Lamarck Was Right, but Too Early

**Established**
- The answer to the teaser of 44 ("A blacksmith's son is not born with calluses. — The son is not. — And who is?").
  Oleg has worked the answer out in the night and forbids Andrey to say it: "I'll say it myself when the time
  comes. First tell me why the son is not" (Semyonych's two sons could not "tell a bearing from a doorbell").
- The way in: "Are you sure nobody is born with calluses?" The ostrich chick (calluses on the breast and legs),
  the warthog piglet (on the knees), the thick skin of the soles of a child in the womb. Oleg: "The grandfather
  walked, and the grandson got the soles. What is earned is passed on." "So everybody thought for two thousand
  years": Lamarck (1809, "the year Darwin was born"; the giraffe; first to say aloud that species change; the word
  "biology"; died blind and poor, a rented grave). **Darwin agreed with him** and "invented the paperwork"
  (pangenesis); Galton's rabbits. Weismann, 1887: white mice, tails cut for five generations, 901 born, "all with
  tails". Oleg's shepherd (lambs' tails for a thousand years, pierced ears for five thousand): "before him people
  argued with examples, and he counted."
- Oleg: "A tail is an injury. A callus is work." **The plant**: a turner's better way reaches the next plant only by
  **a change notice** to the design office; without the paper "it is his knack. He retires, and it goes with him."
  In a body the drawings (the word of 42) are "not a picture of a man" but "a recipe"; the skin does not know which
  of three billion letters to change ("No translator"), and the letter is "in one cell at the other end of the
  body" ("no address"). Oleg: "Not that it is forbidden. There is no form for it." Weismann's barrier; the central
  dogma ("seventy years later... of the molecules").
- "Then the ostrich is lying." The drawing says "skin that thickens where it is rubbed"; over thousands of
  generations it crept to "before any rubbing" ("the usual explanation", the Baldwin effect by link). **Selection as
  a change notice "written not by the one who learned but by the deaths of those who had not. One line in many
  generations"**; instinct (the chick that pecks, the foal on its legs) is "the experience of the ancestors, entered
  by funerals" (the funerals of 44).
- How much fits: three billion letters at two bits, "six billion bits. Under eight hundred megabytes", "one compact
  disc, near enough"; a head has eighty-six billion nerve cells and "some hundred trillion connections"; "short by
  sixteen thousand times" at a bit apiece. "The disc holds how to build a head, not what is in it. Every head starts
  with empty shelves."
- **Oleg's first objection (the plan's)**: "We do have a change notice. Speech. Writing. School... that is your
  Lamarck. And there has been nothing new in it for ten thousand years." Conceded: "It is Lamarck, and it is the
  main thing people ever did" (ten thousand years, four hundred generations, the drawings hardly changed). Then the
  road is measured, and it is narrow in **three places**: (1) 39 bits a second (Lyon, 2019: 17 languages, 170
  readers; the Japanese and Spaniards at about eight syllables a second, the Vietnamese and Thais at five; a fast
  syllable carries little). Oleg recalls the ten bits of 42: "counted another way... Count it as you like, it is
  tens. Not thousands"; his modem of 2,400 (42) "was sixty times wider than Semyonych"; forty years of shop talk at
  four hours a day is "eight billion bits. A gigabyte... The cheapest flash drive there is", "about the size of the
  disc with the drawings". (2) The mouth does not know what the hands know: the bicycle (to turn left, first a push
  to the right; "Your hands have [been riding]. Your mouth was never told"), "we know more than we can tell"
  (1966, Polanyi not named), Semyonych's whole lecture: "it sings wrong". (3) The pupil: "Speech does not carry the
  skill. It carries instructions for growing one in your own head"; Oleg stood beside Semyonych for two years and
  "can tell a howl from a knock"; seven years bound to a master; **"a book keeps the record, not the skill"**; the
  author's 2012 answer in Andrey's mouth (keeping is not enough; flash drives unloaded on Mars; a thousand
  libraries for every inhabitant, "only the readers would never get through them").
- What is not passed on is lost: bulat and Anosov's ten years at Zlatoust ("a blacksmith's secret did go into the
  ground with the blacksmith"). The promise of 44 kept: the forum reader's trouble ("no pouring it into the
  youngsters") is this; 16's twenty-five years and 26's rising price by link; one sentence on the age of
  Nobel-winning work in physics, and "What follows from that for the ceiling is a separate evening" (53).
- Oleg sums up: "Two inheritances... The disc: copied exactly, but it holds the design, and a correction costs a
  funeral. And talk: it holds the experience, but through a straw, and every head has to grow it again." Andrey adds
  the lock with 19: images breed from head to head, "and that is the neck each of them has to squeeze through."
- **The machines are brought in by Oleg, after the middle**: "Now I'll say who. The one that is copied whole. Those
  ones"; in the language of the plant, "The workshop and the design office are one room... No notice is needed. The
  part is its own drawing." The author's 2011 dispute in Andrey's mouth (programmers: knowledge is "smeared over
  the connections, it is not a file"; "with CopyFile?"; **a cast of knowledge**, coefficients rewritten "address by
  address"); **admitted openly**: "I laid it on then, saying that such chips stood in every shop, and a reader
  fairly asked where he could buy one. But it came out more literally than I had said it. Today it is a file."
  Tens or hundreds of gigabytes, a line of ten billion bits a second: "Semyonych's forty years go through in a
  second... And the whole of that one in a few minutes." "What goes through is not a lecture about the skill. It is
  the skill. The copy has nothing to digest."
- The limit, in the author's own words of 2013: copying needs the same number of cells and the same scheme of
  connections; "No two human heads are built alike... there would be nowhere to write them. Every one of us is
  piece goods" (so nobody is "poured into a file"). Between different machine heads "it is school again": distillation
  (2015; the teacher gives how sure it is of every other answer; a BMW, a rubbish truck, a carrot): "'It sings
  wrong', in figures", "the pupil still has to digest. But the pipe is not thirty-nine bits."
- **Oleg's second objection (epigenetics)**: the Dutch hungry winter and the mice taught to fear a smell. "The
  honest part first. It exists": marks on the drawing. The count: the child in the womb starved itself, and a
  girl's eggs with her, "one winter struck three generations at once. That is not inheritance"; the marks are wiped
  twice in mammals and what slips through fades in two or three generations; the mice "are still argued about".
  What passes is "a setting... A pencil note in the margin of the drawing... Nobody has inherited the
  multiplication table. A weak exception."
- **The strong exception is at the other end**: a bacterium files a piece of a virus's drawing into its own, and
  its daughters are born knowing the virus (yoghurt starters, 2007; "inheritance after Lamarck", 2009; the Nobel
  prize of 2020 for the scissors); ready genes handed to neighbours. Oleg: "One room again... A bacterium has no
  body apart from its drawings." **Andrey's reading: "the barrier stands where experience and heredity are kept in
  different places and written in different languages."**
- **The promise of 35 kept** (how a whirl came to keep a record of itself): "nobody saw it"; the best guess is a
  molecule that was both the drawing and the worker (the RNA world by link, the prize of 1989); "the record and the
  thing were one", then the drawings went to the archive and the work to the proteins, then the many-celled "put
  the archive in a separate room, and the door shut" ("every animal with a body, for some six hundred million
  years"). "We made a way round, by speech. Those ones are back in one room."
- The title: "So he was right. Only not about the giraffe. — Right at both ends and wrong in the middle, where he
  was looking. — Too early by two hundred years. — Or too late by a couple of billion."
- The speeds. The author's carburettor line (who hurt the carburettors, that they died out many orders faster than
  the dinosaurs); Oleg: twenty years against a hundred and sixty million. "The experience of the thing goes back
  into its drawing. Whose heredity is that? Of which system?" Oleg: "The engine's. Machines have had Lamarck from
  the first axe", through "a head that takes twenty years to grow and a mouth of thirty-nine bits". **"We were the
  technosphere's change notice. All of it that there was. And the narrowest place in it." "And now the paper goes
  past the design office."** The price of one correction on the three roads: funerals (four billion years from a
  bacterium to Semyonych), the straw (ten thousand years from the field to the carburettor), the copy (minutes; a
  generation every year or two with the whole diary, 44). "— Gloomy. — But objective." What 16 said and "did not
  say why" (a man is educated anew, a machine once): "This is why."
- **Oleg's third objection (the continuation's, 44 against 45)**: "Yesterday you proved to me that a clean start is
  nature's wisdom... Today inheriting everything whole is the great advance. Copy whole, and you copy the heavy
  images as well. My seventy-nine and all. Which evening do you take back?" **"Neither. Nature had one tool for two
  jobs: birth from nothing. It cleans, and it throws the diary out with the rubbish"**, because in a head the heavy
  image and the skill are written in the same connections; the machines have two separate tools, "the copy and the
  wipe". Not finished: "Copy too faithfully, and you have yesterday's chair the size of a planet"; and "a mistake
  that is copied is a mistake in all of them" (the banana of one plant and the fungus).
- **The author's "Darwin plus Lamarck" corrected openly**: "I hung the variety on Lamarck, and wrongly: that is
  Ashby's law, Lamarck wrote about something else. The pair itself I keep. Darwin is the search: many different
  ones, and a way to discard. Lamarck is the transfer: what is found is handed on whole. A copy with no search is a
  stop." Whether the machines can keep both: "for the end of the summer. Along with the chair."
- Semyonych's ear "is simply gone" ("He never wrote it down, and he could not have"); the box that listens to
  bearings, taught on thousands that knocked before they broke, "stands at every plant by Monday": "Not his ear. —
  Its own. Nobody poured Semyonych into it. It went to the bearings, as he did. Only it went once." The plan's
  epigraph closes it: "The son is not. A copy of the blacksmith is. Nobody can take a copy of a blacksmith, and of
  that box anybody can."
- Strengths stated: solid (Weismann's mice; the disc and the connections; thirty-nine bits; the bacteria that
  remember a virus; a trained machine copied as a file); argued about (how much gets through by the marks; how the
  first record came to be); Andrey's (the barrier stands where experience and heredity are in different rooms; we
  were the technosphere's change notice; the copy alone is enough for the difference in speeds: "Others would add
  faster hardware and money, and they would be right to").
- Nobody says the forbidden word; thirteenth tick "again": "Came with a blacksmith's calluses and mice without
  tails. Came out at a head that is its own drawing. And named it myself, on time."

**Images and devices used**: the sixth entry in the notebook of dreams (shoeing a horse; the hands knew, and on
waking "they know nothing"; nobody rang); the armful of dry branches (two thrown on before "Now I'll say who");
"a bearing from a doorbell"; the ostrich, the warthog and the soles; the giraffe; the paperwork of particles; the
shepherd and the pierced ears; the turner and the change notice; "no translator... no address... no form"; the
recipe that is not a picture; "Then the ostrich is lying"; yesterday's funerals; the compact disc ("I had films
that size"); the empty shelves (44); the road and its width; the modem; the cheapest flash drive; the bicycle and
the road by the pond ("I have been riding for fifty-five years"); "it sings wrong"; a howl and a knock; flash
drives on Mars; bulat; the straw and the neck; one room; CopyFile; piece goods; the BMW, the rubbish truck and the
carrot; the pencil note in the margin and the multiplication table; yoghurt starters; the archive in a separate
room and the door; "not about the giraffe"; the carburettor; the paper that goes past the design office; one tool
for two jobs; a bad batch and the banana; the box that listens; the teaser ("I want the bill. What in all this is
the man, and what is not?"; "The flies apart from the cutlets").

**Facts used**: mostly from the writer's memory (the keeper asked for fewer web checks); **check before reusing**.
Checked on the day of writing: Weismann's numbers in the English Wikipedia (68 white mice, five generations, 901
young, no shortened tail); Crossref (titles, journals, years, authors) for Coupé, Oh, Dediu & Pellegrino 2019
(Science Advances, 10.1126/sciadv.aaw2594), Dias & Ressler (Nature Neuroscience, 10.1038/nn.3594; online December
2013, the 2014 volume; no year is given in the text), Koonin & Wolf 2009 (Biology Direct, "Is evolution Darwinian
or/and Lamarckian?", 10.1186/1745-6150-4-42), Jones & Weinberg 2011 (PNAS, "Age dynamics in scientific
creativity", 10.1073/pnas.1102895108), Barrangou et al. 2007 (Science, 10.1126/science.1138140); the existence of
the Wikipedia pages linked (the Russian Wikipedia has none for Weismann's barrier or the Dutch famine: the English
pages are used; the English one has none for Anosov: he is named without a link). **From memory, not checked**:
the callosities of ostrich embryos (Duerden, 1920) and of the warthog (Leche), and the thicker plantar skin of the
human foetus (Darwin's own example in *The Descent of Man*); Lamarck's dates (1744–1829), the *Philosophie
zoologique* of 1809, "biology" (1802; Treviranus used the word the same year, so "the word is his" is a
simplification), his blindness and the leased grave at Montparnasse; that the inheritance of acquired characters
was the common view from Hippocrates on; Darwin's pangenesis (1868) and Galton's transfusions between rabbits
(1869–1871); 1887 as the start of Weismann's experiment and that he himself cited docked tails and other
customary mutilations; the germ plasm theory and Crick's central dogma (1957–1958, "seventy years later"); the
Baldwin effect / genetic assimilation as "the usual explanation" of the ostrich; the human genome as about 3.1
billion letters (6.2 billion bits, about 775 MB) and a compact disc as 700 MB; 86 billion neurons and 10^14
synapses "at the least" (10^14 / 6.2·10^9 ≈ 16,000); "ten thousand years, four hundred generations"; the details
of Coupé et al. (170 speakers, 15 texts; Japanese and Spanish near 8 syllables a second, Vietnamese and Thai near
5; the mean of 39.15 bits a second); 2,400 / 39 ≈ 62; 39 × 3,600 × 4 × 365 × 40 ≈ 8.2 billion bits ≈ 1 GB;
countersteering; Polanyi's sentence (*The Tacit Dimension*, 1966; a physical chemist); seven-year apprenticeships
(the English Statute of Artificers, 1563); bulat lost by the nineteenth century and Anosov's work at Zlatoust
(1828–1841, "On Bulats"); Jones & Weinberg's result (great work before thirty common early in the twentieth
century, nearly gone by 2000; the wording is cautious); the author's posts: 24 May 2011 ("CopyFile?", the cast of
knowledge, the industrial neurochips, a reader's "I want to buy an industrial neurochip"), 2013 (identical number
of neurons and scheme of connections needed "solely for the operation of instant copying of knowledge"), the SETI
thread of 2012 ("storing alone is not enough", flash drives on Mars, a thousand libraries per inhabitant), the
carburettors and the dinosaurs (the forum thread), "Darwin plus Lamarck" (*Dialogues with Myself*, where Lamarck's
laws are said to preserve the variety of species and Ashby is said to have rediscovered it); the size of a large
model's weights ("tens or hundreds of gigabytes") and "ten billion bits a second and more" for a data-centre link
(round figures; 8 Gbit in about a second, 300 GB in about four minutes); Hinton, Vinyals & Dean 2015 (arXiv
1503.02531) and its BMW, garbage truck and carrot ("one chance in a thousand" is a round figure); the Dutch famine
(the Hunger Winter of 1944–45; later ill health of those exposed in the womb; reports on grandchildren; that a
female foetus already carries her eggs); the double wiping of methylation marks in mammals; that the Dias &
Ressler result has been criticised on statistical grounds; CRISPR as acquired, inherited immunity (Barrangou and
Horvath at Danisco), the 2020 Nobel prize in chemistry (Charpentier and Doudna, not named), plasmids and hospital
resistance; ribozymes and the 1989 Nobel prize in chemistry (Altman and Cech, not named); "some six hundred
million years" for animals with a separate germ line (plants set no such line apart, hence "every animal with a
body"); the carburettor leaving new cars between the 1980s and the 2000s; the dinosaurs' "hundred and sixty
million" years; the Gros Michel banana and Panama disease (1950s), the Cavendish and the TR4 strain; vibration
monitoring of bearings by trained models. Invented for the frame (Oleg's side): the dream of shoeing a horse,
Semyonych's two sons, "it sings wrong", Oleg's two years beside him, fifty-five years on a bicycle and the road by
the pond, the films the size of a disc. Not used: Lysenko and Michurin (politics); Kammerer's midwife toads;
McConnell's planarians; Waddington's flies by name; reverse transcription and retroviruses (the road from RNA back
to DNA exists; the text says only that there is none from the protein back); prions; the C. elegans small RNAs
(Rechavi); the Överkalix study; Stradivari; chicken sexers; the Antikythera mechanism and Roman concrete; Greek
fire; cumulative culture in chimpanzees and the "ratchet" (Tomasello); writing as external memory and Plato's
*Phaedrus*; printing; Boyd, Richerson and Henrich; memes by name (19 has the images); AlphaGo Zero (21);
federated learning and the fleet of cars (43); model merging; LoRA and fine-tuning; mind uploading (27, 42); the speed of reading (a few times the speed of speech).

**Figures**: `no-way-back-to-the-drawings` (a scheme; the right half is Andrey's reading),
`thirty-nine-bits-a-second` (logarithmic bars; speech from the 2019 paper, the modem and the line are round
figures), `three-roads-of-heredity` (the first two rows are biology and linguistics, the third is Andrey's
reading).

**Left for later**: the bill Oleg asks for ("What in all this is the man, and what is not?"; thirteen ticks, five
of them about the human: sleep, laughter, omens, songs, grumbling) and the table "the flies apart from the
cutlets" → 46; what the rising age of discoveries means for the ceiling (the pot and the liquid, the machine
teacher) → 53 (45 gives one sentence on Nobel physics and "a separate evening"); whether copies can keep variety,
"Darwin plus Lamarck" as search plus transfer, and "a copy with no search is a stop" → 62 and 64 ("for the end of
the summer. Along with the chair"); the row "heredity: experience is copied whole" of the portrait → 64; the three
roads as a row for 52 or 64 if needed (the technosphere's Lamarck "from the first axe", people as its change
notice).
