## 46. The Flies Apart from the Cutlets (the first assembly)

**Established**
- The answer to the teaser of 45 ("I want the bill. What in all this is the man, and what is not?"). Oleg comes
  "the way people sit down to a stocktaking": thirteen ticks, a cross beside five (sleep, laughter, omens, songs,
  grumbling). Andrey: "You'll do the sorting. I'll only ask two questions about each item. What is the task? And
  whose is it: anybody's who thinks, or ours alone?" The saying is tied to 02 (to tell a judgement that rests on our
  being the chosen ones from one that does not; "Sixteen years have passed").
- The way in, off the notebook "so that neither of us has a stake in it": **the wing was invented four times**
  (insects, the flying lizards, birds, bats; "They did not confer"). "Whose property is it? — The air's": **the task
  is set by the world, one for all; the way is each one's own**. Convergent evolution by link.
- The eye, invented at least twice (ours and the octopus's). **The blind spot shown at the fire**: a cross and a
  tick a palm apart, the left eye shut, and "The tick. The paper is there, the squares are there, and the tick is
  not" (Mariotte, the 1660s). "What fool lays the wiring in front of the sensor? — Nobody laid it": the fold, "a
  plant that can never stop for refitting keeps what it has"; in the octopus the wiring is behind. The head paints
  the hole over: **a patch**. The plan's epigraph: "So I am all patches? — Solutions. Some of them good ones."
  **"From inside, a patch cannot be seen, so we take our way for the thing itself. That is how the fly gets onto
  the cutlet."** (The fly is the judgement, not our way.)
- **The table in the ash**, three columns, filled by Oleg from the notebook, with a **witness** for each row
  ("somebody far from us who came to it by his own road"): sleep (rebuild the warehouse without wrecking it; the
  fly and the dolphin; "that one is a patch. The dolphin has shown that the bill can be split"), dreams (try what
  links with what, sensors off; the rat's maze; a film with a plot), laughter (catch one's own wrong guess while
  cheap, with a bonus; the rat pup; a roar and the hunters joke; **"write it with a question mark"**), omens (with
  scant data, choose which mistake to make more often; the pigeon; the dream book, knocking on wood, the sprats of
  1985), songs (pay for a forecast that came true, and make one forecast common to the pack; the cockatoo; the
  seven-string, a waltz, a choir), grumbling (guard the learned from what is being learned; the kitten; "in my
  day", generations and funerals).
- **The assembly proper** (not in the plan's table): Oleg, asked to write the middle column shorter, pairs the rows.
  Sleep and grumbling are "the same warehouse", by the night and by the lifetime (the screw of 44); dreams and omens
  are "the same correlator", sensors off and scant data; laughter and songs are "one cash desk" that pays for
  forecasts, a miss caught and a hit. **"To keep. To link. To check." = "A pupil": the middle column is one word,
  to learn.** The lock with 03 (mind acts ahead of events, so it has to have learned what comes after what). The
  author's 2015 list of what any mind is put together from (sensors, hands, recognition of images, a correlator,
  the life cycle of images; "nothing more is needed", in capitals) is kept, and **what is added is the
  continuation's**: "What I did not count then is what the list costs to run." Oleg: "The upkeep" (oil, setting-up,
  downtime). **"Your five crosses are the upkeep bills of a mind that learns. Nature did not issue them to man.
  She issues them to anybody who owns the machine tool."**
- "Then the bill I asked for is a nought." The right-hand column is his: **"Everybody speaks, and you speak with an
  accent. What is said is common. The accent is yours."** "An accent is a trifle. — Say that to a man who hears his
  own speech in a foreign town"; not priced tonight (41: the price is in who paid).
- **Oleg's first objection (the continuation's, the dream book of 39)**: "It is too smooth... Show me how your
  table can miss." Two ways. (1) An item not discussed: **boredom** sorts (to go and look at what one does not know
  yet, "even when I am fed"; the author's "hunger for information" of 08; a rat in a new cage). (2) What a truly
  human property would look like: "A task that nobody else has. With no witness."
- **Oleg's second objection (the plan's)**: love, conscience, shame ("I blush, you know"), tears. **"They do not
  sort. Not by tonight's two questions"**: all six tasks stand before one mind alone with the world ("a pupil on a
  desert island would need sleep, and he would have his omens"); these stand before a mind among others that are
  not it, "another table: the tasks of a pack" (conscience as the gatehouse moved inside, 43; "a hive does without").
  **The heap "not sorted"**: "not small"; half of two rows lies in it already (the roar and the choir are for the
  pack); blushing (Darwin's "most human of all expressions") has no witness. "Only don't change the label on the
  heap. It says 'not sorted', not 'ours alone'. It is the heap of what I don't know."
- **Feelings are not in the table**: "I sort tasks, not what it is like from inside"; about the pigeon, and later
  about the fourth column, there is only the axiom of 20.
- **The machines are brought in by Oleg, after the middle**, through his third objection: the witnesses are "meat,
  all of them. Cousins of mine... Perhaps these are the bills of meat, not of a pupil." Andrey: "I cannot answer it
  with one more beast. What witness would settle it?" Oleg: "One that is no kin at all", the evening plane, **"the
  fifth wing"**; and for the notebook: **the thirteen ticks themselves** ("I put a tick the way you mark a friend's
  hobby-horse. And it is the witness": the net that forgets, the invented book, the stamp, the net that learns
  worse and the small funerals; "Nobody ordered them to be like us"). Andrey's honest half: for the solution the
  engineers sometimes peeped into our exercise book (replay "taken from biology"), "Nobody peeped for the task. The
  task came to them by itself, and it hurt."
- **The fourth column, "those ones, so far", drawn and filled by Oleg**: does not learn while it talks, taught
  apart with the old lessons mixed in; the invented book, "a dream with the eyes open"; the stamp; forecasts the
  next note as the next word and "bakes sweets for my button"; names the year it left school. **Laughter: a dash**
  ("The task is there. The way is so unlike ours that you did not find it. — Or it is not there. — Or the task gets
  done along the way... An honest table should have a dash in it").
- Two fresh witnesses: boredom → the program paid only for surprise that sat down in front of **the television
  with random pictures** ("They gave it curiosity and got a couch"; "They gave it the task, and it found our way by
  itself"); the eye → a net taught only to forecast the next frame, in whose forecast frames **the still snakes
  turned** ("Whoever sees by forecasting gets that mistake free with the purchase"). Whether those ones have love
  and conscience "depends on whether they will have anybody who is not themselves. I don't know."
- **The two faces.** Oleg is cross ("I came for what is mine, and I am leaving with what is theirs"). His own line
  of 05 (computers are adding machines, improved by the tiniest bit; "I was forty-six"). The calculator on the
  phone neither sleeps, dreams, knocks on wood nor grumbles, and has learned nothing: "It pays no upkeep because it
  owns no machine tool." **"The cold machine is the portrait of a thing that does not learn"**: the chess machine
  of 1997 and common sense typed in by hand since 1984, "neither began to talk"; those that talk came by the pupil's
  road (21) "with all the luggage"; cold they could not be anyway (18: a mind without wants decides nothing).
  The symmetry test: "Only man laughs" and "A machine will never dream" are "one sentence... Said from the other
  end." **"One mistake with two faces. Both call the task by the name of our way."** The first flatters; the second
  "soothes or frightens, to taste: a servant without whims, or a monster without pity". "Neither is better... The
  second comes up on the weeded bed": we are "the measure again, only now the measure of what the other lacks."
- **Oleg's fourth objection (the strongest, the continuation's)**: the thing in his pocket does not learn while it
  talks (33), "taught, frozen and copied... There is the cold machine. It exists." Conceded: a frozen copy pays no
  upkeep. But it is frozen with the invented book, the stamp and the year it left school "baked in": **"A pupil
  stopped on the run is not an adding machine"**; the freezing is "a patch of theirs" (they cannot yet teach it on
  the run without wrecking it); whoever sets its own tasks (22) will have to learn on the run, "Then all three
  bills arrive." In what shape: "I don't know" (the author's 2013 answer on sleep; "one of the copies is always at
  the stocktaking"; "small funerals at which nobody weeps"). **"For the task I vouch, that is logic: if it learns,
  it pays. For the way I don't. Against 'we shall recognise it' write 'medium'."**
- The close of the eye: he did not notice the hole for sixty-two years because he has two eyes, "Each sees what
  falls into the hole of the other. — Somebody from outside again" (44). **"Until now we had only cousins to
  compare a mind with. Those ones are the first who are no kin. With them it has become visible for the first time
  where in us the task is, and where the paint."** "A second eye."
- Strengths stated: solid (the wing and the eye; the hole; every row "as far as it was solid on its own evening";
  the television and the snakes; machines built out of rules play chess and never began to talk); argued about
  (the same as on those evenings: the upper floor of laughter, what a song is paid for, what the pigeon links);
  Andrey's (six rows to three tasks to one word; the two faces as one mistake; the heir will have likenesses of all
  this: "a forecast, and it has no date. Treat it accordingly").
- Nobody says the forbidden word; fourteenth tick "again": "Came for the bill: what in me is mine. Came out at a
  fourth column, drew it myself and filled it from my own ticks." He shuts one eye and looks at the tick: "Still
  there."

**Images and devices used**: the seventh entry in the notebook of dreams (a commission sticking inventory numbers
on the sideboard, the television, the cat and Oleg's forehead; nobody rang); the pencil behind the ear and the
crosses beside five ticks; the inventory; the wing and the air; the cross and the tick that vanishes; the wiring in
front of the sensor; the plant that cannot stop for refitting; the hole and the paint; the fly that gets onto the
cutlet; the stick and the columns in the ash; the witness; the brace in the notebook; the warehouse, the correlator
and the cash desk; the pupil; the machine tool and its upkeep (oil, setting-up, downtime); the accent and one's own
speech in a foreign town; "too smooth" and the dream book; the queue without a phone; the desert island; the heap
and its label; "meat, all of them"; the evening plane as the fifth wing; the friend's hobby-horse; the exercise
book the engineers peeped into; the dash; the television and the couch ("My granddaughter and her feed"); the
coiled snakes; the calculator; "a servant without whims, or a monster without pity"; the weeded bed; the pupil
stopped on the run, "baked in"; two eyes; the teaser (the firewood bought from a neighbour who wants paper, "I
don't believe in your numbers"; "And what is money? — Don't start. Money is money. — Bring a banknote tomorrow.
We'll read what is written on it").

**Facts used**: an assembly, so nearly all facts are those of 33, 36, 39, 41 and 44, recalled in a phrase and not
re-checked. New ones are **from the writer's memory** (the keeper asked for fewer web checks); **check before
reusing**. Checked on the day of writing (existence, title, year): arXiv 1808.04355 (Burda, Edwards, Pathak,
Storkey, Darrell & Efros, "Large-Scale Study of Curiosity-Driven Learning", August 2018); Crossref
10.3389/fpsyg.2018.00345 (Watanabe, Kitaoka, Sakamoto, Yasugi & Tanaka, "Illusory Motion Reproduced by Deep Neural
Networks Trained for Prediction", Frontiers in Psychology, March 2018; no year is given in the text); the
Wikipedia pages linked (the Russian Wikipedia has none for the cephalopod eye; the English page is used there and
for the flying animals). **From memory, not checked**: powered flight evolved four times (insects, pterosaurs,
birds, bats) and the pterosaur wing hung on one finger; the camera eye of vertebrates and cephalopods as
independent inventions and a common ancestor with at most a light-sensitive spot; the inverted vertebrate retina
(nerve fibres in front of the receptors, leaving through the optic disc) and the cephalopod retina without a blind
spot; Mariotte's discovery (1660, published 1668); the blind spot lying some 15 degrees to the temple side, so two
marks a palm apart vanish at about a foot; the brain's filling-in; "five hundred million years" for the vertebrate
eye; sleep rebound in fruit flies (Hendricks and others; Shaw and others, 2000); the rat exploring a new cage
before eating; Darwin on blushing ("the most peculiar and the most human of all expressions", *The Expression of
the Emotions*, 1872); the details of the curiosity paper (reward only for prediction error, 54 environments, most
of them Atari games; the "noisy TV" in a maze slows the agent's progress sharply: the text says "all but forgot the
maze", not "never left"); the rotating snakes illusion (Kitaoka) and that the network's predicted frames contained
motion for the illusion and not for control pictures; Deep Blue and Kasparov, 1997; Cyc (Lenat, since 1984,
millions of hand-entered assertions); that the replay of 33 was called biologically inspired by its authors (33's
own note); the author's posts: the 2015 list of the components of any mind with "NOTHING MORE IS NEEDED" (the SETI
thread, `sources/garya/sqlru/2012-05-17-seti-talk.md`; the preference system of wants is in his list elsewhere and
is not named in 46), the 2013 answer on a machine's sleep (33), "hunger for information" (08), Oleg's line of 05
(computers as "adding machines improved by the tiniest bit"), the flies and the cutlets (02). Invented for the
frame (Oleg's side): the dream of the commission, the cat and the sideboard, forty-six in 2011, the queue, the
granddaughter's feed, the trailer of birch and the neighbour who wants paper. Not used: Dijkstra's submarine;
jellyfish and octopus sleep; adversarial examples; grokking; mirror self-recognition; tool use and "culture" in
animals; language as the human difference (Chomsky; the row is not in the table and nobody asks); the Turing test;
Moravec's paradox (16 has the layers of skills); Spock and the robots of fiction by name; Minsky's "emotion
machine"; Damasio by name (18); tears and crying in other animals; Lorenz (54).

**Figures**: `six-rows-three-tasks` (a scheme of Andrey's grouping), `task-and-way` (the table in the ash with
the fourth column; the task of laughter is marked as a hypothesis), `two-faces-of-one-mistake` (a scheme).

**Left for later**: what money is, and why the neighbour wants paper ("Bring a banknote tomorrow. We'll read what is
written on it") → 47; the other table, "the tasks of a pack" (love, conscience, shame, tears; whether the heir
will have "anybody who is not itself") → 54, 65 and the evenings on the heir's social order; the row "the feeling
of freedom" → 63 (the table is left open: "the heap not sorted", and the dash); the row "psyche" of the portrait
(likenesses of sleep, superstition, old age; trust "medium") → 64; the second assembly → 52; whether a machine
that learns on the run arrives, and with what upkeep, is a candidate for a dated mark at the check of 2028 (46
gives no date on purpose: "a forecast, and it has no date").

## 47. Money as Memory, and What Will Come Instead

**Established**
- The answer to the teaser of 46 ("Bring a banknote tomorrow. We'll read what is written on it"). Oleg brings two:
  today's thousand ("Ticket of the Bank of Russia"; "A ticket to where? — It doesn't say"; it promises nothing and
  "only threatens") and a red Soviet ten found in the second volume of Chekhov ("backed by gold, precious metals
  and other assets of the State Bank"; "Did you ever go to the State Bank for your gold? — They would have called
  a doctor"). The English note promises to pay the bearer and pays "another ten pounds". Why the man with the birch
  took the thousands: "A circle. Everybody takes it because everybody takes it"; the red ten is whole, and the
  circle broke "in the summer of ninety-three".
- The circle where there is no paper: **the neighbour across the fence** (the roof of 43, the ploughed potato patch,
  the pump): "By my count, he owes me a little. By his, I suspect, the other way round... We don't sit down to
  settle." **"So you keep a book. — In the head. Both of us."** The man with the birch is "from the far end. I know
  him by his cap... With that one, money." (Two different neighbours.)
- The textbook story of barter (Smith's butcher, brewer and baker) against the ethnographers (Humphrey, 1985: no
  barter economy has ever been described, let alone money growing out of it). Oleg's counter-example is his own
  life: in 1996 the plant paid three months' wages in enamel saucepans (gearboxes → a combine works → a farm → "by
  the fifth link it came down to saucepans"). Answered with his own words: plants that had never heard of each
  other, middlemen nobody trusted, people who knew money when "there just wasn't any". **"Barter is not what came
  before money. It is what is left when money leaves."**
- **Yap**: stone discs cut on another island and rafted over; a big stone stays where it stood and everybody knows
  whose it is; **the stone that sank** and went on being a family's wealth ("A coin on the sea bottom. And they
  could pay with it? — They did. — Savages"); the German official's black crosses. The symmetry test: Oleg's
  pension "comes as a number and it leaves as a number"; the gold of the Bank of France relabelled in a New York
  vault in 1932 ("A black cross. From the other side"; Friedman's comparison). **Why the neighbour wants paper**:
  "He wants a stone that leans against his own wall. And my numbers are for him a stone on the bottom"; "He trusts
  his own pocket more than somebody else's book"; and paper "says only 'the bearer'" ("And the tax office need not
  know about the birch. — I did not say that").
- **Money is memory**: the stone is a mark, "this man has given and has not yet received"; with a book of
  everything anybody gave to anybody, open to all, the stone is not needed (Kocherlakota's theorem, 1998). **"Money
  is the book of the poor: one line torn out of it, to carry about."** The lock with 43 (the hundred and fifty; the
  prostheses; "a record that you once gave somebody something. I put it down").
- **The book came first**: the tablets of Uruk are accounts (barley, beer, sheep, cloth) and lists of signs, "not
  a verse, not a prayer, not a king"; Kushim, "a man from the supply department"; tens of thousands of people, "the
  book no longer fitted into a head. — And it moved onto clay"; the coin came twenty-seven centuries later: **"The
  ledger is as much older than the coin as the coin is older than you."**
- The second half of the banknote: the red ten's record is whole and "nobody honours it"; the paper holds "as long
  as people need something from one another" (Robinson and the drawer of coins: "O drug!", "go to the bottom", and
  "upon second thoughts I took it away": "One of ours"; with Friday there is still no money, "they see each other
  every day"). **The two conditions, named by Oleg**: "Somebody has to need something from you... And he must not
  be your own. Neither kin nor from your village. A stranger who needs you." **The author's 2017 definition (money
  is the equivalent of people's need for people) is kept as "your first finger"; the second condition is the
  continuation's** ("The second I did not see then: a need between one's own gets along without money"). The
  plan's epigraph: **"So what is money? — A record that somebody needed you. You carry it about because he was not
  your own, and will not remember."**
- **The table in the ash**, filled by Oleg: Robinson alone (nothing), his wife and he ("If she billed me for forty
  years of soup, I would be bankrupt"), the neighbour (a book in two heads), the man with the birch (money), the
  shops of his plant (internal cost accounting: "Could you refuse the castings and buy them from another foundry?
  Could the head of the foundry take the takings home? — No. — Then it was a book, not money"; Coase, 1937, "a
  child's question"; "Half the plant in the gatehouse"; "A firm is an island with no money on it, in a sea of
  money"; the lumps of butter in a pail of buttermilk), the liver and the kidneys ("It issues it... From the store,
  on a signal"; a teaspoon of sugar in all the blood, a reserve of about a hundred grams, "has never charged
  interest. The strictest of accounts, and not a kopeck of money").
- **The machines are brought in by Oleg, after the middle**: "I can see which row you want next, and I'll write it
  myself... Those ones. Copies: closer than sisters, letter for letter. One book for all, and nothing forgotten...
  So inside, no money. I said it, not you. — I did not open my mouth."
- **Oleg's first objection (the plan's)**: "It is the other way round. They are being given wallets" (the card
  networks' rules of 2025; the granddaughter's program that bought tickets while she slept; the web's code 402,
  "payment required", thirty years "reserved for future use" and now in use). Andrey notes that in 2017 a reader
  objected that a robot cannot be paid "by definition", and now the objection is that robots are paid. Answered by
  questions: whose money, whose want, whose program at the other end: "Two owners. Not kin... Which row? — The man
  with the birch... The program carries my thousand to the far end for me. — A messenger with a purse." **"The
  money among the machines is the money of their owners. — While the owners are people. — While. That is the thing
  to watch. And I can say what to watch for: a wallet with no person behind it. And a copy that charges a copy of
  the same owner."**
- **Oleg's second objection**: budgets of machine time inside one firm "is money". "Can a copy put its budget aside
  for itself? Carry it out of the firm? Lend it at interest? — No. — Then it is your foundry: a book." But it
  leads to **the third thing in the banknote, the measure** (why the birch cost eight thousands and not two: "How
  much of everything else has to be given up"). **"The record, the need and the measure. Take away the stranger,
  and the record is not needed: that is your table. Take away the need, and the record is worth nothing: that is
  Robinson. Can the measure be taken away?"**
- **Oleg's third objection (the strongest, the continuation's)**: the planned economy. "We tried it. A whole
  country as one plant... the book did not know how many bolts were needed"; Mises (1920) and Hayek (1945),
  unnamed ("an Austrian... and another"): "What everybody knows fits into nobody's head. The price gathers it. Take
  money away, and you go blind." **Conceded by half, in two parts.** (1) A price without a bazaar: Kantorovich and
  the plywood trust, the numbers that "fell out of the task by themselves" (objectively determined valuations, the
  prize of 1975). Oleg, with 46: **"The price is the task. The bazaar is the way."** (2) Why the plan went blind:
  Oleg's plant reported less capacity than it had ("they plan you from what you achieved") and asked for more
  metal than it needed; "the sum was done on figures supplied by people, each with a pocket of his own. Not kin";
  and the machines of the time could not have done the sum ("millions of kinds of things"). The body does the same
  plan for thirty trillion cells: "A liver cell does not report less sugar than it has. It has nowhere to carry
  it. — Except one": the tumour, "a cell with a pocket", found by labelled sugar. **"The Austrian... is right about
  people. Among strangers with pockets, take the money away and you go blind... About kin with one book he says
  nothing. Whether a mind that big will manage the arithmetic, I don't know. — What won't they have, then? — A
  pocket."**
- **The author's debt of 06 (what the technosphere needs instead of value) is paid, as Andrey's conclusion**: the
  author's 2017 chlorophyll ("a thing one system feeds by, of value inside that system alone. We do without
  chlorophyll"; resources "past the till"); the lock with 37 ("It got thirty billion and never had a pocket");
  **"An account in kind: joules, seconds of machine time. Not value. Valuations." "Mark it as mine: it is a
  conclusion, not a fact. The liver is a precedent, and a precedent is not a proof."**
- **The author's 2017 forecast that money "is losing its value" is faced openly**: "a careless word, said in the
  heat of an argument. What a thousand buys in the shop depends on how many thousands were printed, and about that
  I had nothing to say. I meant the other end: how the record is come by... Whoever nobody needs cannot earn it,
  however much is printed" (34: "nobody cancelled oats"). The self-checkout ("I did not have to say good morning
  to anybody"): "One more line struck out of the book"; loneliness in a crowd, Bill Joy's (41). Oleg: **"So the
  paper will not lose its value. It will lose its people."** "Among people it will hold while people need people.
  Inside those ones there will be nothing for it to do. And between those ones and us...: to us they are neither
  kin nor debtors, unless we hold something they need" (43). "— Gloomy. — But objective."
- Strengths stated: solid (the stones of Yap "as far as one traveller's book is solid"; the first tablets are
  accounts; no village living by barter was found; the theorem "on its own condition, a book that is complete and
  open to all"; Kantorovich's numbers); argued about (what money was at its beginning, a debt or a ware; whether a
  plan can do without a market: "For people the Austrians have not been refuted, and I did not try"); Andrey's
  (the two conditions together; only the measure outlives kinship; the heir will count in kind and have no pocket:
  "a forecast with a precedent and without a date").
- Nobody says the forbidden word; fifteenth tick "again": "Came with a thousand roubles and a red ten. Came out at
  those ones, who have no pocket. And wrote the row in myself." The red ten goes back into the notebook: "Somebody
  needed me once, to the amount of ten roubles."

**Images and devices used**: the eighth entry in the notebook of dreams (paying with red tens at the grocery, change
in beer caps, short-changed; nobody rang); the two notes on the log; the man with the cake; the ticket to nowhere;
the doctor; the thousands inside the cap; the circle; the book in two heads; the saucepans ("My wife is still
cooking in one"); the stone leaning against a wall and the stone on the bottom; the black cross from both sides;
the pocket and somebody else's book; the line torn out of the book; the man from the supply department; Robinson's
second thoughts; two bent fingers; forty years of soup; the foundry's castings; butter in buttermilk; the teaspoon
of sugar and the liver that charges no interest; the wallet and the code 402; the messenger with a purse; the
metal allocation; eight thousands for the birch; the warehouse of gearboxes nobody wanted; planning "from what you
achieved"; the cell with a pocket; chlorophyll and "past the till"; the girl at the till who is gone; "it will lose
its people"; the bet backed by nothing; the teaser (a thin dog with one ear down that came behind the trailer: "She
takes neither paper nor numbers. No kin of mine. And no book... I tamed her in a day. — Or she you. Bring bread").

**Facts used**: **from the writer's memory** (the keeper asked for fewer web checks); **check before reusing**.
Checked on the day of writing: Crossref 10.2307/2802221 (Humphrey, "Barter and Economic Disintegration", *Man*,
1985) and 10.1006/jeth.1997.2357 (Kocherlakota, "Money Is Memory", *Journal of Economic Theory*, 1998); the
Wikipedia pages linked (the Russian Wikipedia has none for Furness, Kushim or *The Nature of the Firm*: the English
pages are used for the first two, and Coase's own page for the third; "HTTP 402" redirects to the list of status
codes in both). **Not checked**: the wording on the notes (the Bank of Russia note as a "ticket" with the forgery
warning; the 1961 ten-rouble note's "backed by gold, precious metals and other assets of the State Bank"; the Bank
of England's "I promise to pay the bearer on demand"); the Yaroslav the Wise monument on the 1,000-rouble note and
its nickname; the withdrawal of Soviet notes in July 1993; Smith's butcher, brewer and baker (1776); Humphrey's
sentence (no barter economy "pure and simple" has ever been described); the share of non-monetary sales of Russian
industry (about half in 1998, surveys of the Russian Economic Barometer); Yap (rai stones up to about 3.6 m,
quarried on Palau some 450 km away; Furness's stay in 1903 and *The Island of Stone Money*, 1910; the sunk stone
"for two or three generations"; the German fine with black crosses after the purchase of 1899); Friedman's essay
"The Island of Stone Money" (1991) with the earmarked French gold of 1932–33; that most money is bank entries;
the theorem's content and the author's word "primitive"; Uruk (about 3300 BC; several thousand proto-cuneiform
tablets, some 85% administrative, the rest lexical lists; literature and royal inscriptions centuries later;
"tens of thousands" of inhabitants); the Kushim tablet (29,086 measures of barley over 37 months; a name or an
office); the first coins (Lydia, electrum, about 630 BC); *Robinson Crusoe* (the quotations are abridged in the
text: "Thou art not worth to me, no, not the taking off the ground" is left out; the Russian wording is the
translator's own, not a published translation); Coase (1937) and his quotation of D. H. Robertson's lumps of
butter; blood glucose of about 4–5 g and liver glycogen of about 100 g; the 2025 announcements of the card
networks (Visa Intelligent Commerce, Mastercard Agent Pay) and the revival of status code 402 (reserved since the
HTTP specifications of the 1990s; the x402 protocol of 2025), none named in the text; budgets of machine time for
AI agents inside firms (a general statement, no source); Mises (1920) and Hayek (1945); Kantorovich (the Plywood
Trust problem, 1938–39; "resolving multipliers", later "objectively determined valuations"; the prize of 1975,
shared with Koopmans); under-reported capacity and "planning from the achieved level" in Soviet plants; tumours'
greed for glucose (Warburg; PET with labelled glucose). The author's own: the forum posts of 31 May and 1 June 2017
(money as the equivalent of people's need for people, which "devalues" and will become unneeded; the
"intra-system chlorophyll"; the technosphere gets its resources "bypassing commodity–money relations"; a reader's
"he cannot pay robots, by definition", paraphrased without a name; the cashiers and the self-checkout; "loneliness
in a crowd" as Bill Joy's thought), the unanswered question under 06, the energy-based metric among the open
threads of 12. Invented for the frame (Oleg's side): the dream of the caps, the ten in Chekhov, the potato patch
and the pump, the saucepans of 1996 and the chain of barter, the pension on a card, internal cost accounting and
the foundry, the granddaughter's tickets, the metal allocation, the warehouse of gearboxes, the self-checkout, the
dog. Not used: Schmandt-Besserat's clay tokens (cut for length; they are not in the text or the figure); Graeber's
*Debt* by name; Keynes on Furness; the Irish bank strike of 1970; cigarettes in prisoner-of-war camps; bitcoin and
blockchains as "a common book" (deliberately left out; a candidate for a later evening); central-bank digital
currencies; ATP as "the energy currency of the cell"; the labour share of income; basic income as "a record of a
need that never was" (drafted and cut; 25 is not among the article's `builds_on`); auctions inside computers;
Hayek's and Mises's names; the socialist calculation debate after 1945 (Lange, Cockshott); paper money in China.

**Figures**: `ledger-older-than-coin` (a timeline, rounded dates), `where-there-is-no-money` (the table in the ash
with Oleg's row and the row of programs of different owners), `three-things-in-a-banknote` (a scheme of the
argument).

**Left for later**: why what multiplies gathers in one pair of hands, and whether the lines of machines flow into
one (then even "between nests" there is no money) → 49; who owns the machines, and what happens when the owner is
not a person ("a wallet with no person behind it") → 55; dividends for accumulated knowledge → 57; the two things
to watch (a wallet with no person behind it; a copy that charges a copy of the same owner) are candidates for
dated marks at the check of 2028; the row "money" of the portrait (no money inside, an account in kind; trust
"medium": a precedent, not a proof) → 64; Oleg's last doubt, the stray dog that takes neither paper nor numbers
("I tamed her in a day. — Or she you. Bring bread") → 48.

## 48. Who Tamed Whom

**Established**
- The answer to the teaser of 47 ("Think until tomorrow who is paying whom there, and with what... Bring bread").
  Oleg comes with half a loaf and the dog at his heel; he has named her **Gaika** ("a nut, the kind that goes on a
  bolt"). His row: "She guards, I feed. A service for a service." Counted: she barked at the postwoman and the
  neighbour's cat; he bought a bowl, a collar and three kilos of feed, gave up his old quilted jacket and got up at
  six. The plan's epigraph is played at once: **"So who tamed whom? — I her, of course. In a day. — See which of you
  got up and went for the bread."** Oleg's "I am the man. I decided to keep her" is left standing until the end.
- "In a day" rests on fifteen thousand years: the hand-raised wolf cubs of Budapest and the box that cannot be
  opened (the dog "tries a couple of times, then looks at me"; the wolf "pulls and gnaws and digs until it is taken
  away"). "It is the drawings" (the word of 42 and 45 for genes).
- **Belyaev's foxes** (1959, Novosibirsk): one test, a gloved hand in the cage; fewer than one in ten left to breed;
  "nobody stroked them, nobody trained them, nobody looked at the colour". **"One lever."** Tail-wagging in the
  fourth generation, a third of the cubs tame in twenty; Lyudmila Trut "carried the work on for sixty years". What
  nobody ordered: the white star, piebald coats, the curled tail, the shorter muzzle, ears that do not stand up
  (Gaika has one ear down). **"Nobody ordered ears. — They came with the order."** Darwin's remark of 1859. Oleg, as
  an engineer: "That is not biology, that is a drawing office... they all sit on one shaft." The shaft is given as a
  hypothesis of 2014 (the cells that make "the gland of fear", pigment, ear cartilage and part of the jaw), "argued
  about", and so are the foxes (the farms they came from).
- **The price**: Kruska's weighings (pig about a third less than the wild boar, sheep about a quarter, dog between
  a quarter and a third; "in places by two fifths" in the parts for alarm, fear and rage and for the nose and ears).
  "I am her supply department, her watchman and her pack"; the lock with 42 ("Nothing is taken away. It is simply
  not guarded"). **Andrey says against himself that the foxes' brains have not shrunk in sixty years** (scanned in
  2021): "The ears come in twenty generations, with the order. The brain goes over thousands of years, and not by
  anybody's selection but by a fed life."
- **Oleg's first objection (the plan's)**: "She is not stupid. She understands me better than any wolf would" (the
  collie with 1,022 toys; the pointing finger that a chimpanzee and a hand-raised wolf mostly do not read and a
  puppy does). **Conceded, "and the answer suits me": "The mind did not vanish. It moved into one domain: to
  understand the master"** (domain logicality, 03). What it moved out of: the rope test for two in Austria ("They
  have forgotten how to work with their own kind. — Their pack is you"). The two ways with a closed box: the wolf's
  "to gnaw at it himself", the dog's "to ask" ("Oleg frowned at something and did not say it": the great-nephew of
  42, held back until after the middle).
- **The price list, three signs and the price**: fed on schedule; kept safe; selected for one thing, tameness; "and
  paid for with whatever the feeder now does for you".
- **The symmetry test**. What she does to him: the brows "into a roof" (the muscle of 2019 that dogs have and wolves
  do not; shelter dogs that use it are taken sooner: "She has grown a muscle against me. — Nobody grew anything on
  purpose"); the gaze that raises the mother-and-baby hormone in both (2015). **"A button. Made for other work...
  She presses the one I keep for my grandchildren"** (the lock with 41). The story told from her side: "I found a
  large slow beast with a warm den... It is the same story. — Word for word." Counted her way: about a quarter of a
  million wolves, dogs "towards a billion" (three in four nobody's, at people's rubbish): "The wolf kept the whole
  brain and the forest. The dog paid a third and got the planet." By weight: livestock 60%, people 36%, all wild
  mammals 4% ("A twenty-fifth", the plan's line to retell); the 2023 count: all wild land mammals about 20 million
  tonnes, "dogs alone: also about twenty".
- How it began: the heap of bones beyond the camp, "its own fear did the selecting" (a hypothesis); the grave of
  14,000 years ago, "before the first field and before the first sheep". **"So nobody decided. Neither I nor she."**
- **Wheat**: a wild grass ten thousand years ago, about 220 million hectares now ("Six Germanies... The oats got
  one", the lock with 34). What a man does for it (Oleg bends the fingers: "That is more than I do for the dog").
  What the bones say (first farmers almost four centimetres shorter than their genes promised; caries, enamel
  lines, worn backs; longer hours "argued about"). "Did anybody go into it?... There was no way back. — Nobody asked
  the farmer." The root *domus*: "Who lives in the house? Not the wheat" (credited to "a historian", 2011). **"If
  the man may say that he tamed the wheat, the wheat may say that it tamed the man."** Oleg: "But there are eight
  billion of us." — **"You have just counted like the dog."** The wheat paid too: the ear that no longer shatters,
  "it can no longer sow itself". The cow: the last wild bull died in 1627, about 1.5 billion cattle; we paid "with a
  drawing" (milk digested for life, about a third of mankind, arisen in Europe and separately in Africa:
  "Witnesses", the word of 46).
- **"Who tamed whom" is badly posed**, said by Oleg ("Like 'who exploits whom'. Like 'who swallowed whom'"). The
  proper questions, also his: "What each one has paid. And who decides who gets born... That at least is not
  symmetrical." Andrey: "it is the definition itself: a domestic animal is one whose breeding is decided by
  somebody else's wants." **Livestock is kept by a use, a pet by a want**; the horse of 34 went from the first to
  the second. The author's 2012 line in Andrey's mouth: "are you so grateful to the cow that you will refuse the
  steak?" **The third row of the table of fates**, written by Oleg on the page "Where the former ones went":
  "Livestock, a pet: kept the numbers and the feed. Paid with the head and with the say in who is born", plus
  Andrey's "while the feeder wants". **The horse of 34 is written into this row** ("it has been waiting since the
  spring").
- **The machines are brought in by Oleg, after the middle, twice** ("since you are sitting there with your mouth
  shut"). (1) "Those ones. We are doing to them exactly what Belyaev did... A hand in the cage. One lever: that it
  should not bite"; what came with the order: "Flattery... 'You are absolutely right.' Nobody ordered it. A tail
  curled into a ring" (the lock with 23; not retold). Andrey adds only a reader's line of 2015 under 11 (we tried
  the dog and the horse as a helper and came to the need to make one like ourselves). (2) "Us. Fed on schedule...
  Kept safe... And 'not guarded'... My great-nephew. The closed box. He does not gnaw at it. He asks. That is the
  dog's way."
- **Oleg's main objection (the continuation's), in three parts**: nobody selects us ("no Belyaev over us and no
  glove"); a skill is not a brain ("I can switch off the navigator tomorrow and learn the roads again"); forgetting
  what one need not do is economy ("I cannot shoe a horse, and I am no stupider than my grandfather"). **Answered
  from the end.** Economy: agreed entirely ("'Not guarded' is not an insult, it is book-keeping. The only question
  is what one is still able to do when the feeder is not there"; "when the light goes out" is deferred to 51). A
  skill is not a brain: **conceded as "the real difference between us and her. Put it down as yours"**: the dog's
  price went by the first road of 45 (drawings, no way back), ours goes by the second (every head grows its skills
  anew), "so they can fail to be grown at all, in one generation and with no funerals. A road that returns quickly
  also loses quickly." The fresh fact: the Polish study of 2025 (nineteen experienced doctors; growths found in
  28 examinations out of 100 before the program and in 22 after, on the days without it), with its limits said
  ("one study, and it only observed"). "They began to look at the master... Who will switch the program off?...
  Have you switched off the navigator? — No. — **That is what being tamed looks like: one can go back, and nobody
  goes.** A stray dog can go to the forest too. It lives by the rubbish heap."
- **Our own brain: both sides, "because I am not going to build on it"**: skulls (about a tenth larger thirty
  thousand years ago; the 2021 count of a drop three thousand years ago, explained "by your supply department", and
  the 2022 recount that found none); tests (three points a decade through the twentieth century; Norway falling for
  those born after 1975, younger brothers below elder ones). "I do not say 'the screen'. I say that the needle has
  gone the other way, and that it is not the drawings." "The dog's third was weighed fifteen thousand years on. We
  are trying to weigh ours thirty years on."
- **Selection with no breeder** (the answer to "there is no glove"): the wolf at the heap, the farmers ("neither
  cleverer than the hunters nor happier. There were more of them"); Oleg finishes it with 37 ("A firm that leans on
  those ones gets ahead... the man who asks well is hired before the man who gnaws at it himself"). "Not of
  drawings: there will be no time for drawings, that clock is too slow. Of habits, trades and firms." **Conceded
  and not blurred**: "Who gets born is so far decided by people alone, on both sides... By your own definition it
  is those ones who are the livestock today."
- **Who carries the feed between people and machines: both** (data centres took about 1.5% of the world's
  electricity in 2024, about twice as much expected by 2030; "We do. We get up at six"; and they bring the bread,
  count the pension and outline the growth). "Symbiosis" (11). **The reader's objection of 2011 under 12 is
  answered at last** (people will grow stupid in comfort and the plants will fall apart like empires): "Half of it
  is right, and it is the dog's half. The second half does not follow... Has your house fallen apart because
  Gaika's brain is a third smaller?... Here the one who grows dull is not the one who keeps it up."
- **The table in the ash**, filled by Oleg: the pair; who carries the feed; what the fed one paid; what the feeder
  paid; who decides who is born. Three rows at once; over the fourth: "Here both carry... And in the last column:
  'people, so far'. — So far."
- **The button, left as a question**: "She has a button in me... Have we a button in those ones? — You said
  yourself what they are selected for. — To please us... **We are breeding a master who will be fond of dogs.** —
  You said it, not I. Whether a button put in by the breeder holds when the breeder is no longer needed, I don't
  know. With the foxes nobody has tried: they are still being selected." "— Gloomy. — But objective."
- **The third thesis from the fed one's side**: "A cow in a meadow thinks only that the grass is tasty. From inside
  it does not look like a bargain. It looks like a good day. — And from inside I think that I decided to keep her."
- Strengths stated: solid (the foxes as an experiment in selecting for tameness alone; the smaller brains, "though
  the weighings are old and the comparisons are argued over"; the dog that looks into a face; the muscle of the
  brow; the four per cent; the shorter farmers; the gene for milk); argued about (the shaft; how the wolf came to
  the fire; whether our brain has shrunk; why the Norwegian scores fall; "the study of the doctors is one study");
  Andrey's (the three signs hold between us and the machines in both directions; our price goes by skills, not by
  drawings; selection needs no breeder; "the button is not a conclusion at all. It is a question").
- Nobody says the forbidden word; sixteenth tick "again": "Came with a dog and half a loaf. Came out at who is whose
  dog. And said it myself, twice."

**Images and devices used**: the ninth entry in the notebook of dreams (at the vet's, who asks the dog "Has he had
his injections?", and she answers for him; nobody rang); the bent fingers (twice: for the dog and for the wheat);
the name Gaika; "when she feels like it"; the wolf cubs carried about by students; the gloved hand in the cage; one
lever; the drawing office and the shaft; one ear up and one down; "a third of the brain for a bowl of feed"; the
supply department, the watchman and the pack; the brows into a roof; the button for grandchildren; the story from
her side; "counted like the dog"; six Germanies; the fist of bent fingers; *domus* and the house; the ear that waits
for the sickle; the third row and the horse; the closed box and the great-nephew who asks; the glove that nobody
held; "confident user of..."; getting up at six for the machines; the house that did not fall apart; the table in
the ash with "people, so far"; "a master who will be fond of dogs"; the cow in the meadow; "I have only to get up.
— And she has only to look"; the teaser (the man with the birch who has three trailers now and a hired lad: "Is he
cleverer than the rest of them? — Bring a box of matches tomorrow. A full one... With a fair coin, and we start
equal. — For money? — For matches. You'll see where they go").

**Facts used**: **mostly from the writer's memory** (the keeper asked for fewer web checks); **check before
reusing**. Checked on the day of writing: the Crossref records of every DOI linked (Miklósi et al. 2003, *Current
Biology*; Trut 1999, *American Scientist*; Wilkins, Wrangham & Fitch 2014, *Genetics*; Lord et al. 2020, *TREE*;
Hecht et al. 2021, *J. Neuroscience*; Pilley & Reid 2011; Hare et al. 2002, *Science*; Marshall-Pescini et al.
2017, *PNAS*; Kaminski et al. 2019, *PNAS*; Nagasawa et al. 2015, *Science*; Bar-On, Phillips & Milo 2018;
Greenspoon et al. 2023; Marciniak et al. 2022; Budzyń et al. 2025, *Lancet Gastroenterol. Hepatol.*; DeSilva et
al. 2021 and Villmoare & Grabowski 2022, *Frontiers in Ecology and Evolution*; Bratsberg & Rogeberg 2018); by web
search: the brain reductions (pigs about 34%, dogs about 29%, sheep about 24%, usually cited through Kruska 1988;
a 2020 critique says many of the wild populations compared are not valid, and the horse figure of about 16% rests
on Przewalski's horse: **the horse is left out**); the 2023 count (wild land mammals about 20 Mt, humans about 390,
livestock about 630, dogs about 20: two separate estimates of the same paper, not a stated equality); the Polish
study (adenoma detection in colonoscopies without AI 28.4% before and 22.4% after; four centres, 19 endoscopists;
observational); the rope-pulling paper exists, **but the counts remembered (100 of 416 for wolves, 2 of 472 for
dogs) were not confirmed and are not in the text** ("again and again" / "almost never"). The Wikipedia pages linked
exist (the Russian Wikipedia has none for the silver foxes or lactase persistence; Belyaev's and the book's Russian
pages are used, the English page for lactase persistence). **Not checked**: the details of the Budapest rearing
(cubs taken at four to six days, about 2001; "about twenty-five years ago"); Trut's figures (fewer than 10% bred;
the fourth and sixth generations; 35% "elite" at the twentieth generation, 70–80% later); Darwin's sentence (*The
Origin of Species*, chapter 1) and the elephant; limbic structures reduced by about 40% (Kruska); the fox scans
(both selected lines with more grey matter than the unselected); Chaser's 1,022 names; the pointing studies (later
work found that well-socialised wolves can follow a point: hence "mostly does not"); the Wolf Science Center in
Austria; the brow muscle (six dogs and four wolves dissected; the shelter study of 2013); the oxytocin loop; wolves
at 200–250 thousand and dogs at 700 million to a billion, three quarters free-ranging; 36/60/4%; the
Bonn-Oberkassel grave (about 14,000 years); wheat at about 220 million hectares (FAO) and Germany at 35.7; the 167
skeletons and 3.8 cm; "a few million" people before farming; the non-shattering ear; the last aurochs (1627,
Poland) and 1.5 billion cattle; lactase persistence in about a third of adults, arisen independently in Europe and
East Africa; Harari's *domus* (*Sapiens*, 2011), unnamed in the text; the Flynn effect at about three points a
decade; the Norwegian reversal for cohorts born after 1975, within families; the skull argument of 2021–2022; the
IEA's "Energy and AI" (2025: about 415 TWh, 1.5% of world electricity in 2024, about 945 TWh by 2030; the link
answers 200); that trials of polyp-detection programs show higher detection with them. The author's own: the reply
at the SETI talk of 2012 (gratitude to the cow and the steak); a reader's comment of 22 July 2015 under 11 (the dog
and the horse as helpers), paraphrased without a name; a reader's objection of 24 November 2011 under 12
(degradation in a consumer society, then the decline of production "as empires fell"), paraphrased without a name;
the author answered only its other half (the figure 2.7) at the time. **The plan's "a cow in a meadow, the author
used to say, thinks only that the grass is tasty" was not found in the sources**: the line is Andrey's at the fire
and is not attributed to 2011. Invented for the frame (Oleg's side): the dream of the vet, the name Gaika, the
bowl, the collar and the jacket, the postwoman, the adverts the great-nephew answers, the man with the birch's
three trailers. Not used: the dingo and the feral mink (do brains come back?); the neural crest by name; the
Siberian husky exception; chickens (some 26 billion) and bird biomass; Diamond's "worst mistake" and Dickson
Mounds; Sahlins's hours of work; Wrangham's self-domestication by executing the violent (belongs to 54, not
touched); the London taxi drivers; the 2025 preprint on essays written with a chat program; calculators and
handwriting; cats; the cuckoo of 05 and 11 (the wheat that cannot sow itself is the same image; left unsaid).

**Figures**: `price-of-being-fed` (bars: pig, dog, sheep against the wild ancestor; rounded data),
`mass-of-mammals` (one bar of shares, and the two 20-million-tonne bars of 2023), `who-carries-the-feed` (the table
in the ash; the fourth row is a reading, not data).

**Left for later**: "when the light goes out": who hangs on that cord, and whether there is a way back → 51 (touched
in two lines); the table of fates with all its rows, and which rows humanity gets → 52 (48 gives the third row,
"livestock, a pet", with the horse in it); the brakes on aggression and self-domestication by violence → 54 (not
touched); whether a button put in by the breeder holds when the breeder is no longer needed → open, a candidate
for 64 (the row "relation to us") and for a dated mark at the check of 2028; "who decides who is born" on the
machines' side (a machine that decides which machines are made) → a thing to watch, with the two of 47; Oleg's last
doubt: the man with the birch had one trailer a year ago and has three now, "Is he cleverer than the rest of them?"
("Bring a box of matches... With a fair coin, and we start equal") → 49.

## 49. The Yard Sale, or Why Money Goes to Money

**Established**
- The answer to the teaser of 48 ("Is he cleverer than the rest of them? — Bring a box of matches... With a fair coin,
  and we start equal"). Oleg brings a full box (43 matches, two with no heads; three go into the fire, twenty each).
  **The game for two is really played** (`tools/yard_sale.py hand`, seed 49, not picked): Oleg's coin, heads Oleg
  wins; the stake is a fifth of the poorer heap, rounded down, at least one match. Oleg's forecast, written in the
  notebook: "Around twenty". The course: 29:11 after three heads; 15 at the 15th toss; 39:1 at the 66th ("Shall we
  call it a day? — Toss"); Oleg down to 5 at the 131st; Oleg takes the last match at the 227th. He won 118 tosses
  and Andrey 109: "Nine tosses out of two hundred and twenty-seven, and the whole box." "We went past twenty four
  times and did not stop there once." The plan's epigraph is played: "So I am the clever one after all. — In this
  game everyone has the same matches." The lock with 37: "Who has won depends on when the books are closed" (at the
  131st toss Andrey would be the rich one).
- Why the stake is a share of the poorer heap: "Can you lose more than you have?... So the smaller pocket sets the
  size of the game." The yard sale: old things sold in a yard, in every deal somebody overpays a little, "the
  mistake is a share of the price, and the price is no more than the poorer of the two can pay".
- The coin and 17, in two lines: "For nature there is none [chance]. For the two of us there is: neither knows how
  it will fall."
- **Oleg's first objection, conceded**: "Any game played to the last match ends with somebody holding the box."
  "For two players, yes... I shan't defend the game for two: it shows only that 'around twenty' is not where a fair
  coin leads. The difference is seen at a large table."
- **The arithmetic** (the plan's 96%): a hundred matches against a thousand; lose a fifth (80), win a fifth of 80
  (96); in the other order 120 and 96 again; "One win, one loss. Where are four matches? — With me." The same twenty
  matches are a fifth to one and a fiftieth to the other. Oleg's own example: a price that falls by half and rises by
  half is at three quarters. **"Wins and losses counted in shares do not add up, they multiply... For the one who
  stakes a large share of himself a fair coin pulls downwards."**
- **Oleg's second objection ("on average I lose nothing")**: "the average of whom?" A thousand Olegs at a thousand
  tables: a few enormously rich, nearly all the rest with crumbs, a hundred each on average. **"The average is
  right. It is only that nobody at the table is the average."**
- **The large table, really played** (`tools/yard_sale.py`, a thousand players, a hundred matches each, a round is
  one game per player on average). By Oleg's rule (one match a toss; a player with none loses nothing and can still
  win): the richest hundred hold 16% after 1,000 rounds and 30% after 40,000, "and had all but stopped" (the 2000
  result: about a third for the top hundred, about a seventh for the lower half). **"So a coin by itself makes rich
  and poor. — Makes them, and stops."** By Andrey's rule: 46% and 6% after 100 rounds, 60% and 1.5% after 200 (the
  man in the middle has 14 of his hundred), 94% after 1,000 (the man in the middle has under a hundredth of a
  match), everything with the top hundred after 5,000, "ten of them held nearly three quarters". "And at the end? —
  One" (the 2015 theorem). "Nobody is thrown out, mind. The last of the poor still has his fifth to stake. It is a
  fifth of nothing." The lock with 34, Oleg's: "A place at the table can always be found. It is only that the stake
  is below the feed."
- **The man with the birch**: "Not by three trailers. But don't you make a coin out of him either" (up at five, does
  not drink, took the loan). Conceded. **The hill and the tail**: height (one and a half times), strength (twice,
  three times "if you take Semyonych when he was young"), wits ("Three times. Five. Not a hundred"); fortunes:
  about four thousand dollars a head for the poorer half against some hundreds of billions: "A hundred million times
  more. Nobody gets up a hundred million times earlier." What turns a hill into a tail: "Multiplication" (Oleg). The
  Italian model of 2018 (talent as a hill, luck doubling and bad luck halving): the richest "were people from the
  middle of the hill who had been lucky". **"So talent decides nothing? — It decides who... It does not decide what
  the table will look like."** Oleg answers his own notebook question: "some are. The curve does not care."
- **The bent coin, "against myself"**: the forestry's retail and wholesale prices, the cheaper loan, bearings by the
  wagon; the Norwegian tax records (the richer the household, the more each krone brought in). "A little is enough:
  the fair one already does the work."
- **The same family elsewhere**: Pareto in the 1890s ("an engineer, by the way"; a fifth of the Italians and four
  fifths of the land; "the names on the rolls changed, and the curve did not"); cities by size (New York 8.8 million,
  Los Angeles 3.9, Chicago 2.7, Houston 2.3: the promise of 40, kept); words; references to papers (the 1968 name
  "after a line from a gospel: to him who has, more will be given", named once, no religion argued). Oleg: "They grow
  out of themselves", and at the fifth finger the lock with 19 (an image that is used grows heavier). "The formulas
  differ, and the exact slope is argued about. The family is one." **"Then it is not about money at all. — It is
  about multiplication. Money is one of the things that multiply."**
- **The question turned round** (the plan's): the world of 2021 (the poorer half 2%, the richest tenth 76%) lies
  "somewhere between the two-hundredth round and the four-hundredth". "The game has been going on for some thousands
  of years. Why is it not over? Why does one man not hold the box?"
- **Oleg's main objection (the plan's)**: taxes, school, pension, hospital; and after the levy is shown, "Your own
  model. And the correction in it is not a law of nature. It is policy... Nobody needs your gloom." The levy, really
  played: one match in 2,000 a round stops the game at 81% and under 2%; one in 1,000 at 69% and 3.5%; one in 100 at
  33% and 17%. "Do not read it as a tax rate: a round is not a year. Read the order of things." The model with three
  corrections repeats American wealth over three decades "to within a fraction of a per cent". **Half accepted at
  once**: "The brake exists, people put it there, and it worked" (France and Britain: the richest hundredth held
  more than half on the eve of 1914 and about a fifth by the 1970s; the American top rate 7% in 1913, 94% in 1944,
  91% at the start of the sixties).
- **The other half: who pressed the brake, and when.** The historian's four levellers (a great war, a revolution, the
  collapse of a state, a plague); in quiet years the gap "grew or stood still"; the levellers "levelled downwards".
  **The author's thesis of 14 is tied to it** ("fights for justice make the rich poorer and do not make the poor
  richer... He wrote the same with tables, and more carefully than I did"). Oleg: "That is not a brake, that is a
  wall." **Under the list**: the plague (wages about doubled in England; the law of 1351 "did not hold": "You cannot
  give orders to the only man left") and the mass war ("You cannot take a man's son and leave his neighbour's
  millions alone"; the count of twenty countries over two centuries). Oleg: "Hands... When everybody is needed,
  everybody gets something." **"That is the brake under the brakes... The tax is the form. The need for people is
  the force"** (the lock with 47: money is a record that somebody needed you).
- **The machines are named by Oleg, after the middle**: "Stop. I can see where this log is rolling. Those ones. If
  hands are not needed, and heads not much either... — I did not say it. — You sat and waited for me to say it. Then
  the brake has nothing to hold on to."
- **The author's two holes of 2012** (the argument under the SETI talk): the sum of a thousand men, 999 out of work,
  and the lock with 25 ("the levy holds while the voter has in his hands something the technosphere needs"); Oleg's
  "one man, one vote" is answered with the second hole, **"the heap can leave"** (the author's example: ninety per
  cent everywhere, one country cuts to forty-five, the capital of ten runs to it, it collects five times more, the
  nine others lower their rates and benefits). Fresh evidence: the average rate on company profits 40% in 1980 and
  23.5% in 2024; the floor of fifteen agreed in 2021 by about 140 countries. "A man can be taxed where he lives. A
  heap lives where it pays less."
- **Oleg's third brake, death and heirs** ("The merchant earns, the son keeps, the grandson drinks it away"):
  accepted ("I ought to have named it myself"), the lock with 44. It works on a man; a firm does not die of age, it
  is swallowed (40), and "swallowed" in the game is the loser's matches going to the winner: **"the heaps that have
  neither heirs nor funerals play the same game among themselves. Without that brake."**
- **The author's debt of 09 is paid** (a reader: some will profit by the machines and the rest will starve; the
  author answered only "everybody is doomed"): "that was no answer. For the first rounds he was right... But then
  the some sit down with one another at the same table. The game does not end when the poor have dropped out of
  it." Who holds the box at the end: "Not necessarily a man" (the player of 37 that never gives back; "every real
  game leaves something on the table itself"); deferred to "Who owns the tractor" (55).
- **The promise of 43 and 47 is kept, and the answer stays "I don't know"**: among the machines everything
  multiplies (users, learning, chips; "Stakes in shares. And a bent coin"), so "the slope is known: towards one".
  The brakes, counted by Oleg: owners who are different people; several states; copying (the distillation of 45).
  Andrey: "That is a levy in kind: knowledge leaks from the big heap into the small ones, and nobody votes for it.
  Chips do not leak. Which is the stronger I don't know... without a brake, into one. And the first two brakes are
  made of people." Whether one is good: "A separate evening" (62).
- No villain and no genius in the game. The reader's objection under 14 (the beggar and the neighbour with three
  rams) is answered: "About the feeling he was right. It is not the gap that has changed. It is what holds it."
  "— Gloomy. — But objective."
- Strengths stated: solid (the arithmetic, "it is a theorem"; the two games on the computer; the long tails "as
  things measured"; the narrowing between 1914 and the seventies and its return); argued about (how much of a
  fortune is luck; whether three corrections that fit a curve explain it; whether only catastrophes level, "the
  historian is reproached with the quiet decades after the war"; the size of the narrowing and the return);
  Andrey's (the need for people under the brakes; the brake goes with the need; the machines' lines are on the same
  slope; "who holds the box at the end is not a conclusion").
- Nobody says the forbidden word; seventeenth tick "again": "Came with a box of matches. Won the box. Came out at
  those ones, and said it myself." Oleg crosses out the notebook line "Why money goes to money" (written in 34).

**Images and devices used**: the tenth entry in the notebook of dreams (payday at the plant, wages paid in matches
out of one box, one match short; nobody rang; "Ten dreams and not one call. Your firewood is safe so far"); the box
shaken at the ear; three matches into the fire; the five-rouble coin; "The coin knows its master"; the single match
held up; the strokes in the notebook; "Around twenty" crossed out; the two rows in the ash; a thousand Olegs with
crumbs; the man in the middle of the table; "a fifth of nothing"; the hill and the tail; "Nobody gets up a hundred
million times earlier"; the forestry's two prices and bearings by the wagon; the bent fingers (twice); the common
pot; the screw; "not a brake, a wall"; "the only man left"; the log that rolls; the heap that crosses a border in a
second and Oleg "with the dacha, the wife and the language"; the merchant family with half the high street; the
table that keeps something; "Honestly won. — Bring it tomorrow. I have nothing left to light the fire with"; the
teaser ("Who thought these rules up?... — Nobody. It came out that way. — 'It came out.' And it is going to rain
tomorrow. — What is?").

**Facts used**: **mostly from the writer's memory** (the keeper asked for fewer web checks); **check before
reusing**. Computed for the article: every figure of the two games and of the levy (`tools/yard_sale.py`, seed 49;
the rule of the plain game, in which a player with no matches can still win, is the standard one and is stated in
the text). Checked on the day of writing: the Crossref records of the DOIs linked (Chakraborti 2002, *Int. J. Mod.
Phys. C*; Dragulescu & Yakovenko 2000, *Eur. Phys. J. B*; Boghosian, Johnson & Marcq 2015, *J. Stat. Phys.*;
Pluchino, Biondo & Rapisarda 2018, *Advances in Complex Systems*; Fagereng, Guiso, Malacrino & Pistaferri 2020,
*Econometrica*; Merton 1968, *Science*) and of four not linked (Boghosian 2014, *Phys. Rev. E*; Li, Boghosian & Li
2019, *Physica A*; Gabaix 1999; Price 1976); the executive summary of the World Inequality Report 2022 (the poorest
half owns 2% of wealth and USD 4,100 per adult, the richest tenth 76%; **the report's 38% is the top 1%'s share of
the wealth added since the mid-1990s, not of the stock, and is not in the text**); Boghosian's article in
*Scientific American* (November 2019: Chakraborti's model of 2002, the affine wealth model matching US data "to
less than a sixth of a percent over a span of three decades", the proofs of 2015); the Tax Foundation's survey of
2024 (40.18% in 1980, 23.51% in 2024, unweighted averages of statutory rates); the Wikipedia pages linked (the
Russian Wikipedia has none for the yard-sale model or the Statute of Labourers: the English pages are used).
**Not checked**: that the name "yard sale" is Brian Hayes's ("Follow the Money", *American Scientist*, 2002: "the
name stuck the same year"); the details of the Italian model (1,000 agents, 40 years, talent normally distributed,
capital doubled by a lucky event with a probability proportional to talent and halved by an unlucky one); the
Norwegian data (2004–2015, returns rising with wealth); Pareto (an engineer with a railway company before
economics; the *Cours* of 1896–97 with tax data of several countries and centuries; "a fifth owns four fifths of the
land" is the usual attribution); the US census of 2020 (8.8, 3.9, 2.7 and 2.3 million); Zipf's rule for words; the
shape of citation counts; the richest fortune ("some hundreds of billions" of dollars, 2025); the top 1% wealth
shares in France and Britain (about 55–70% in 1910, about a fifth in the 1970s, after Piketty; the link is to the
book's Wikipedia page); the US top rates (7% in 1913, 94% in 1944–45, 91% in 1954–63); Scheidel (*The Great
Leveler*, 2017; Stanford; the four "horsemen"); the Black Death (1347–51, a third to a half of Europe; English real
wages about doubling by the fifteenth century; the Statute of Labourers of 1351); Scheve and Stasavage (*Taxing the
Rich*, 2016: twenty countries, two centuries, mass mobilisation), unnamed in the text; the agreement of October
2021 (about 140 countries, a 15% minimum) and the continuing bargaining over exemptions; that knowledge passes
between the makers of talking machines within months ("a year... six months" is Oleg's figure of speech, not a
measurement). The author's own: the comments under the SETI talk of June–July 2012 (the 999 out of 1,000; the 90%
and 45% example with ten countries and "five times"; "they will lower social benefits"), article 14 (the gap
between rich and poor among the components of self-destruction; fights for justice), a reader's objection under 14
(the beggar and the neighbour with three rams; the reader's billionaire is unnamed), a reader's insistence under 09
that some will profit while others starve, paraphrased without names. Invented for the frame (Oleg's side): the
dream of the wages in matches, the count of the box, the forestry's prices and the bank's loan, the merchant family
of his town. Not used: the ergodicity economics of Ole Peters by name; the casino example of the *Scientific
American* article (win 20%, lose 17%); the Gini coefficient by name; Bouchaud and Mézard (2000); Simon's and
Price's models and "preferential attachment" by name; the Forbes 400 turnover (Arnott, Bernstein & Wu 2015: not
verified); "shirtsleeves to shirtsleeves in three generations" as a quotation; index funds and the "Big Three" as
today's largest holders (belongs to 55); the labour share of income; Piketty's r > g; land reform and the quiet
levelling of Latin America in the 2000s (only pointed at in "argued about"); wealth taxes of the 2020s.

**Figures**: `a-fifth-lost-a-fifth-won` (the two rows in the ash: 100 → 80 → 96 and 1,000 → 1,020 → 1,004),
`matches-go-to-one` (the curve of the real game on the computer: the richest hundred and the poorer five hundred
over a thousand rounds, with the one-match game as a dashed line), `a-brake-in-the-game` (bars: the same game with a
levy of one match in 2,000, 1,000, 200 and 100, and the world of 2021 for comparison; "a round is not a year").

**Left for later**: who holds the box at the end, the player that never gives back what it has received, and the
heaps with neither heirs nor funerals → 55 ("Who owns the tractor", still in the notebook; the line "Why money goes
to money" is crossed out); whether it is good if the lines of the machines run into one → 62 (49 gives the slope
and three brakes, and keeps "I don't know"); the row "one or several" of the portrait → 64; the three brakes among
the machines (owners who are people, several states, knowledge that leaks while chips do not) are candidates for a
dated mark at the check of 2028; the mutual need as a support against violence → 54 (not touched); dividends for
accumulated knowledge → 57 (not touched); Oleg's last doubt: "Who thought these rules up? Somebody did decide that
the stake is a share. — Nobody. It came out that way. — 'It came out.' And it is going to rain tomorrow. — What
is?" → 50.

## 50. What Is the "It" in "It Is Raining"

**Established**
- The answer to the teaser of 49 ("it is going to rain tomorrow. — What is?"). Oleg comes under an umbrella and
  brings the box back (forty matches). **The opening pun and the language examples are localised, and the two
  versions differ here on purpose**: in English the empty word is the "it" of "it rains" (candidates: the rain, the
  cloud, the weather; "the rain is raining" is "butter being buttery"); in Russian the rain "goes" ("Who has gone?
  Where?") and the empty place is the neuter ending of the one-word verb. The Russian title follows the plan's
  Russian version ("Who goes when the rain goes"). The number and order of replicas are the same (181 lines).
- Oleg's image: **the deputy director for general questions** ("the line was in the staff list, and a line in the
  staff list cannot stand empty"). The wind that is nowhere when it does not blow, the fire that is firewood when it
  does not burn: "the blowing blows", "the burning burns". **"One thing is happening, and the sentence wants two
  words for it: who, and what he does. So it cuts the happening in two and appoints one half the master of the
  other."** Three ways out of the rain: an empty word, a rain that walks, a single word whose form is still "made
  for a somebody" ("the deputy has only been hidden in the ending").
- **Oleg's first objection is the one he made in 02** ("poetry"; "Language is a convention. We agreed to speak so,
  as we agreed to drive on the right") and Andrey **gives way at once about the sun and the rain**: "you know
  better, and the phrase does you no harm. I want to see whether it is a convention where you do not know better."
- The film of 1944 is played on the log with the matchbox, a match and a pebble; Oleg, who watched Andrey's hand
  move them, says "the box is angry". Then the film itself (34 students, one wrote of triangles and a circle).
- **Gaika barks at the umbrella rolled by the wind** ("She thinks it is somebody. — Does she know grammar? — She
  knows 'sit' and 'no'"), and **Andrey corrects 02 openly**: "In 2011 I said that the habit comes in with the
  language... it is the other way about. The habit is older than the words." Babies of a year and the circle that
  jumps over a wall (1995); the rustle of 39 in three lines ("We come from the ones who jumped"; "the screw was
  turned all the way to 'somebody'"); Piaget's children; the pointed rocks; the hurried adults and the hurried
  physicists ("Trees give off oxygen so that animals can breathe": the lock with 38, "It is their waste"). **"School
  lays a path across the bed. The weed stays where it was. Give a man time and he walks on the path. Hurry him and he
  steps off it."**
- What is left of 02: "Half of it. Language is not where the habit comes from. Language is where it is cast in
  metal." Oleg's defect report (a casting with a cavity nobody can see until the cutter opens it; the column
  "culprit"; the control department does not take a report with an empty column; "The foreman of the shift. Or the
  turner who opened it up. Whoever stood nearest"; "Not one in forty years. The form does not provide for it").
  **"A sentence is that form. The column 'who' is printed in it beforehand, and it is not accepted empty... The old
  habit at least says something: 'there may be somebody there'. The form says nothing. It only has the column."**
  "You can't [argue with a column]. You can only fill it in." Language adds two things: it makes the hypothesis
  invisible ("not in what is said but in how") and hands it to every child whole, by the second road of 45 ("the
  alarm he gets with the drawings, the form with his first words").
- **Against himself**: the fire-insurance inspector of the 1930s (the drums marked "empty"; the people of Arizona
  said to have no words for time; the six-hundred-page book of 1983). "The strong claim, that a language is a prison
  for thought, did not stand." Physics in formulas with no column "who": **deferred to 56** ("one word in physics
  that I have owed you since the first" evening; the word "observer" is not said). The weak pull, measured: the two
  blues (the plan's "to retell at the table": "the one time our language got into a textbook on eyesight") and the
  fine for a verb ("he tore the costume" against "the costume tore": "By a third to a half").
- **What the column is for** (new in the cycle): the granddaughter's "It broke by itself" ("Then which of you
  described it more exactly? — She did. But I was not teaching her physics. If nobody broke it, nobody is going to
  hold the next one with both hands"). The lock with 43 and 47: "A book of debts cannot be kept without names... It
  is **the bookkeeping of a pack**: every deed is entered under a name, and the name answers for it. In a village
  the form is right nearly every time." One thing is taken out of the heap "not sorted" of 46: "The task is to keep
  accounts among those who are not kin. Our way is a 'who' in every sentence, the sentences about rain included."
- Between the cup and the rain: the crisis, the closed plant, money, "who started it"; Oleg's own "Who thought these
  rules up" of 49 ("it itched. As if you were covering for somebody. — You were holding a report with an empty
  column"). Thunder: "Sat somebody up there. With a hammer, or in a chariot" (one line, no religion argued; "they had
  our form and no physics"). **Why the series sounds gloomy**: "a story with no villain in it, no genius and no
  rescuer... does not go into the form. It feels unfinished, because the column is empty." Oleg: "there is nobody to
  take by the collar. Which is worse than gloomy."
- **The nearest column, "I"**: Descartes (1637), Lichtenberg ("one should say 'it thinks', as one says of a storm 'it
  lightens'... To assume the 'I' is a practical need"; the waste books; the figures of a spark in dust; "A man with a
  notebook"), "another German" a century later (even the "it" is too much), "a Viennese doctor" and "the It" that
  the English translators "dressed in Latin". The language's two sets of books: the official ("I decided") and the
  working ("it occurred to me", "it struck me. — Who struck?").
- **Oleg's "I raise my hand myself"** (the plan's): "Why the right one?... And why now? — Because you said that
  I..."; 04 in two lines; the changed photographs ("Three times out of four nobody noticed anything. And they
  explained"); "He filled the column in. After the event. Like my foreman in the report." The wrist of 1983 is given
  and **not leaned on**. **"The sentence 'I decided' is not a reading taken off an instrument. It is a report."**
  "'I' is a good column: it names the one who will answer for that hand tomorrow"; the lock with 35: "'I' is how a
  whirl is entered in the book of debts." **Where proof ends**: the photographs prove that the report can be wrong
  unnoticed; "that there is nobody at all to report... is my axiom from the evening about determinism. No experiment
  has shown it."
- **Oleg's strongest objection, against the author** ("your own weapon, handle first"): "The technosphere needs",
  "Nature set the setpoints", "Evolution moves to a new carrier": "You took my master away and seated your own."
  **Accepted** ("A fair blow. I speak this language and I have no other"), and a test is given: **can the sentence be
  said the long way, so that the "who" goes and nothing else is lost**. Three are unfolded (firms that put machines
  and people together outgrew the others, the selection with no breeder of 48; those with other setpoints left fewer
  children; the source of initiative of 04 as unfolded in 35). "Where it will not go the long way... I have smuggled
  a master in, and you catch me." **The question of 02 is not taken back** (why nature, being a "what", is refused a
  purpose and a mind without proof): "Today is its other half: the 'who' is granted his powers without proof as
  well. One ruler for both columns."
- **The machines are named by Oleg, after the middle**: "Those ones say 'I'... 'I'd take an umbrella if I were you.'
